# Quotes by topic

## [[game-ideas-and-genres]]
- "Game development is not about big ideas most of the time. It's mostly about very diligent execution of a lot of small ideas." ([Roblox 2018-07 15:42](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=942))
- "I think there is a huge roblox market niche for really intense melee combat games just like there is for first-person shooters." ([Roblox 2018-07 29:12](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1752))
- "Creativity isn't about having new ideas, but rather good ones." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-game/287493))
- "I have no doubt in my mind that this will never be a front page game but it's not intended to be so." ([Last Level Studios 2020-03 27:24](https://youtu.be/n0ODJH9EgWc?t=1644))

- "Roblox is a platform so vast and large that you only need a tiny percentage of the daily concurrent playerbase in your game to be earning millions of robux." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578))
- "it's solving problems" ([Roblox 2022-09 0:24](https://youtu.be/ImH6XYqNJUs?t=24))
- "I'm just going for the most gamebreaking possible ideas here because that always goes great" ([ItsZyonic 2023-12 8:13](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=493))

- "it's cool to see people bring their own unique spin to an idea that someone else brought into the world" ([Daiorg 2025-02 9:01](https://youtu.be/nutv8RT5UrQ?t=541))
- "Good artists copy but great artists steal." ([PineappleDev 2025-03 0:17](https://youtu.be/IEAr0I_sUV4?t=17))
- "every simulator has SPS well I'm breaking the cycle instead of pets I will make boxing gloves" ([janisjanis01 2025-03 6:21](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=381))
- "Compact and focused games which you can finish in a short space of time without losing your mind." ([AlvinBlox 2025-03 1:54](https://youtu.be/dtRGRx-2WNg?t=114))
- "On Roblox quality means fun, retention, addictiveness, a game people can play over and over without getting bored." ([SmartyRBX 2025-03 0:26](https://youtu.be/_wqZyaTEsHQ?t=26))
- "They start with the player fantasy. What does the player want to feel?" ([SmartyRBX 2025-03 1:21](https://youtu.be/GPP0DzJGpXc?t=81))
- "I was building a game around what I thought was fun, but I wasn't building what players crave." ([SmartyRBX 2025-04 0:42](https://youtu.be/N2FND74tgGE?t=42))

- "The secret to making an influential game isn't to follow trends or to make an extremely ambitious project, but it's to just make something original." ([Daiorg 2025-08 21:45](https://youtu.be/GkKUU9mCgwc?t=1305))
- "Roblox runs on a series of video game genres. A hub with mini games off the side of it is not one of those genres. So you're not going to get algorithm traction." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 33:02](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1982))
- "The only reason why the template games, for example, work is because they are just reusing game loops that are platform proven to be engaging." ([bubDEV 2026-01 0:32](https://youtu.be/2lPsxPtj2mM?t=32))
- "Games without fail states are growing faster than any other genre on the platform right now." ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/top-10-roblox-games-by-players-march-2026-data-breakdown))
- "The cheapest way to advertise your game is by making a game that lots of people want to play." ([CallMeDogey 2026-03 4:46](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=286))
- "if you make a realistic FPS game, well, don't do that, please. Nobody's going to play it." ([CallMeDogey 2026-03 5:27](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=327))
- "We're looking for games that push aesthetic boundaries and make players think 'Wait, that's Roblox?'" ([GamesBeat 2026-03 ](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/))
- "There's a lot of very simple games that have been made in about a week that have done very, very well." ([VuukStudios 2026-03 1:46](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=106))
- "A lot of people are going to play that, not even going to lie." ([VuukStudios 2026-03 12:17](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=737))
- "We found it very fun to just play duels with each other and just fight PvP. Genre-wise, duels wasn't really a thing, especially on Roblox at the time." ([Roblox 2026-04 0:44](https://youtu.be/IuJWzpvpIa8?t=44))
- "A lot of the popular games were already existing games but on different platforms." ([janisjanis01 2026-04 0:40](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=40))
- "Bandit beaters... these are probably way less effort to create and basically get the same amount of players as TD games." ([1_Bruce 2026-04 4:48](https://youtu.be/iGng-GC_xFk?t=288))
- "Explain your game to a complete stranger in one sentence. And if you cannot do it, consider your game fully dead." ([rasdev 2026-05 0:11](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=11))
- "find a niche game with an underserved audience... has a proven core loop, but tons of bugs, people love the game, but they're complaining about it" ([Tizzy 2026-05 9:17](https://youtu.be/BNdHkeKRUdE?t=557))
- "Quiz genre is a great one to look into. Decent amount of players, low competition, very easy to develop." ([Tizzy 2026-05 4:49](https://youtu.be/RQe-MLgY_Zc?t=289))

- "You never ever copy ideas one to one. You get multiple ideas and you figure out how to combine those ideas to make something brand new." ([rasdev 2026-05 3:25](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=205))
- "If you can combine those two ideas, you're going to create something so brand new, you're going to dominate these charts." ([rasdev 2026-06 1:51](https://youtu.be/q0uePCbBlk0?t=111))
- "Purple ocean equals high demand, but poor execution." ([Tizzy 2026-06 12:18](https://youtu.be/dUmh6GAvJ-E?t=738))
- "The only ones that survive are the ones that mutate, okay? Different title, different thumbnail, underserved market." ([Tizzy 2026-06 8:09](https://youtu.be/dUmh6GAvJ-E?t=489))
- "Pick a niche with real demand and build something into it with passion. Or make it unique." ([rasdev 2026-06 5:28](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=328))
- "We need both amazing casual games and deeper games; we want the algorithm to assign value to both." ([Roblox Creators 2026-06 23:33](https://youtu.be/B3R1ybI3yzs?t=1413))
- "The first step is you have to find the gap, not what to copy." ([rasdev 2026-06 0:21](https://youtu.be/TqjyNV9La5c?t=21))
- "Make sure you steal like an artist... get multiple inspirations like two or three and combine into one." ([rasdev 2026-06 4:57](https://youtu.be/TqjyNV9La5c?t=297))
- "Think about how can I just make this game 20% better." ([Tizzy 2026-06 17:04](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=1024))
- "Go to the Roblox charts and look at what's growing right now, not what's already huge." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 0:16](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=16))
- "Don't build what you think is cool... you got to build on a proven concept on this new Roblox platform." ([rasdev 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=0))
- "Nine times out of 10, that is just not the case... the things that blow up are based off of other things that already blew up." ([rasdev 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/fZJEDcibS_M?t=0))
- "Games where you just click buttons to make numbers go up." ([Raul 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=0))
- "If you copy these games to the exact same game, nine times out of 10, you will not blow up." ([rasdev 2026-07 3:27](https://youtu.be/t3i54NTcDi8?t=207))
- "Picture a 7-year-old kid trying to fall asleep at night thinking about wild scenarios; give players the chance to fulfill those fantasies." ([Tizzy 2026-07 0:43](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=43))
- "You need to be the solution. So, you need to be the arbiter, aka the solution, towards a certain outcome." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 13:06](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=786))
- "If you make a totally original game, the chance of you blowing up are kind of hard compared to if you just copy what works" ([rasdev 2026-08 4:05](https://youtu.be/Ox5pwUuzF-M?t=245))
- "Good artists copy, great artists steal. Lazy copying is the enemy, not copying." ([rasdev 2026-08 1:01](https://youtu.be/17DGro3FUlM?t=61))
- "I want to make a restaurant game where you start with basically nothing and slowly build your restaurant into a huge business." ([LovJak 2026-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/0RZeCn3-XKg?t=0))
- "Going after the clickers was like a humble... nobody was even scared of it. It way over performed." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-08 8:17](https://youtu.be/UWem4l0swO8?t=497))
- "The question is no longer about quality, it's about something else. It's about perfectly optimizing the game for the highest possible profit." ([MonarchDev 2026-08 1:41](https://youtu.be/_DwOE49GDdo?t=101))

- "Your game idea actually caps the amount of CCU you can peak at." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 5:04](https://youtu.be/eS0Vc7h0VW0?t=304))

- "All you really need to do is change at the bare minimum 20% of it. This is all you need to make meaningful cash on Roblox." ([Tizzy 2026-09 14:21](https://youtu.be/CRCcsYEB_6A?t=861))
- "Same game play loop, different themes. That's like the best strategy on Roblox." ([kra_DeZ 2026-09 6:21](https://youtu.be/ZradA-cygP0?t=381))
- "Your game needs a 30-second explanation, it's not going to win." ([rasdev 2026-09 2:06](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=126))

- "Would you do you think a kid's going to play a horror game that would scare the heck out of them? ... They beat it once, and they never come back to it." ([kra_DeZ 2026-10 9:05](https://youtu.be/dE0njfPNsdY?t=545))
- "Game idea, not going to lie, is 90% of the work, bro." ([VuukStudios 2026-10 2:56](https://youtu.be/DTJeR2gJSZo?t=176))

## [[trend-research-and-validation]]
- "If it's working on Fortnite again it'll probably also work on Roblox." ([1Coal 2023-08 3:53](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=233))
- "The truth is, no one knows if something is going to sell, but you can get some good ideas." ([Roblox Creators 2025-04 7:04](https://youtu.be/Zb1BJow0NV4?t=424))
- "it's only working because GrowGarden has such a large presence on Roblox that it's basically carrying the game's momentum right now." ([Quez 2025-06 2:48](https://youtu.be/WOG3Rttf-V4?t=168))
- "I think it's because it's a meme obby and they are just like trending when the meme's trending." ([Arow 2025-07 1:09](https://youtu.be/KsaRtStNO3s?t=69))
- "Good artists copy, great artists steal. Study this guy's channel and see what works." ([janisjanis01 2025-08 9:06](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=546))
- "Q1 2026 results, expected around April 30, will offer the first real data point on which version of 2026 we're living in." ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-roblox-still-growing-in-2026-a-data-driven-look-at-the-numbers))
- "Developers watching trending games and building adjacent experiences is becoming a viable strategy." ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026))
- "Breakout means the game recently started growing at an unusual rate and hasn't even peaked yet." ([rasdev 2026-05 2:10](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=130))
- "Look for comments like 'I wish I could play this game. Where can I play this game?' These are signals of demand." ([Tizzy 2026-05 12:34](https://youtu.be/8q6kdFVL_fY?t=754))
- "Simpler games were blowing up. Algorithms were favoring what most call slop. If I wanted to blow up... I would have to sacrifice my dream game." ([Duckable 2026-05 18:48](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1128))
- "I look at is clear demand signals usually off platform." ([Tizzy 2026-06 1:42](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=102))
- "I saw that through Road Trends, which is like a stat sort of statistic. Their monetization was pretty poor, and then they basically took everything and copied us." ([VuukStudios 2026-06 12:01](https://youtu.be/nvnSmeP3_xM?t=721))
- "Viral games aren't built on cool ideas that pop into somebody's head. They're built on ideas that are proven to be working." ([rasdev 2026-07 2:03](https://youtu.be/t3i54NTcDi8?t=123))
- "In the trending games, there's no natural disaster game. So what if I make my own?" ([Raul 2026-07 0:43](https://youtu.be/kTUl4ZwjS4c?t=43))
- "Your market needs to meet what you're making. A great idea won't hit the algorithm if the market doesn't want it." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 12:15](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=735))
- "28% of the games in the top 100 by visits and 33% of the games in the top 1,000 were launched within the preceding 90 days." ([Roblox 2026-09 4:53](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=293))
- "The real question you need to be asking is what do people already obsess over and what is annoying about the way they currently do it." ([rasdev 2026-09 2:43](https://youtu.be/ILhTEWqz2kU?t=163))
- "we categorized every player by their avatar... we went inside of games and we looked at their avatar and who they're actually targeting." ([Alex Builds Games 2026-10 8:42](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=522))
- "There's probably like two games about this trend. So, this is one way." ([kra_DeZ 2026-10 2:45](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=165))
- "Steal a brain rot and grow a garden are down significantly. Steal an egg is up. Same mechanic, different skin." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 10:15](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=615))

## [[core-loops-and-game-design]]
- "Try not punishing the player but reward them for the behavior you want." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838))
- "You don't want to be the tic-tac-toe of a melee combat game because everyone knows how to not lose a tic-tac-toe and it's just not fun." ([Roblox 2018-07 16:49](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1009))
- "Stagger is such an important feedback tool for saying hey I messed up I should stop doing whatever I just did." ([Roblox 2018-07 12:33](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=753))
- "It's never about telling the player where you want to go; it's about using your environment, your assets and all those choices to drive them there." ([Roblox 2018-07 30:44](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1844))
- "Focus on the core game loop first... as long as the core concept is fun to play then you're onto a winner." ([Last Level Studios 2019-09 43:22](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2602))
- "Your game should be easy to learn, but fun to play." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805))
- "Replayability comes from the idea that the value of the second playthrough won't be the same as the previous playthrough." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534))
- "Allow the players to improve if they do something different." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534))
- "At least 75% of that game's revenue is generated by only 1-5% of its player base." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-game-monetization-guide/1235207))
- "Tumor is a system that directly conflicts with your core loop or competes for player attention with your core loop." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 15:54](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=954))
- "A season pass is a feature that runs for a set duration of time... it facilitates that core loop." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:11](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=71))
- "If your players can't find it, if they don't know where it lives in the UI, they're not going to engage with it. So surfacing is really important." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 13:39](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=819))
- "It's always easier to make a feature easier over the span of it actually being live, than more difficult." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:17:07](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4627))
- "We walled off a lot of the roleplay experience to their houses and kind of like dropping them into this empty world." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 19:13](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=1153))
- "They need short term goals they can achieve, not just long term grind." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 16:11](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=971))
- "If I'm just asking the player to throw a party, it doesn't mean that anyone else is required to show up." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 26:08](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=1568))
- "If you're not enjoying it then chances are absolutely no one else is gonna enjoy it." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 4:32](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=272))
- "core gameplay gets less fun the more you play it" ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 3:11](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=191))
- "If you add content without any progression, your superfans are going to blow through the content right away, and they're just going to want more." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 14:35](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=875))
- "Will some classes be stronger than others? Sure. However, it is important to note that each class should hold an almost equal advantage." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-balance-the-pillars-of-balance/1585238))
- "If you don't pay attention to your core loop and you don't make good fun inherently, your game is not going to be fun." ([Roblox Creators 2022-01 0:18](https://youtu.be/bROouMt1b3g?t=18))
- "Those mobile players are going to be struggling the heck out of that exploration feature; keep the region concise but explorable." ([Roblox Creators 2022-03 1:21:13](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=4873))
- "If you want to create difficult decisions for the player, have fewer currencies that can be spent on lots of different things." ([Game Maker's Toolkit 2022-04 5:53](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=353))
- "By adding a sense of risk and reward, we not only go some way towards fixing the exploit, but we add extra decision making." ([Game Maker's Toolkit 2022-04 13:37](https://youtu.be/Zrf1cou_yVo?t=817))
- "it actually like massively reduced day one retention so maybe people were running past each other" ([Roblox Creators 2022-04 0:47](https://youtu.be/5N84f1ZuwRc?t=47))
- "If you build something that doesn't feel authentic to them that can be a real turn off." ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:09:32](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=4172))
- "always let the player conclude never give them the whole thing even if you give them 95 of it they should reach the last 5 on their own" ([Roblox Creators 2022-06 27:19](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=1639))
- "it's kind of like that dopamine hit, just constantly feeling like you're making some kind of forward progression" ([Roblox Creators 2022-08 2:07](https://youtu.be/lJAEKxoIUAs?t=127))

- "Quests provide players with fresh goals and incentives, which can motivate them to play longer and more often, increasing their engagement." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design))
- "doing so opts your game out of future script updates and you'll need to manually merge changes" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines))
- "Season passes are not general experience passes. General passes are one-time purchases that confer gameplay bonuses." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design))
- "Automatic rewards run the risk of being overlooked or misunderstood. Forcing players to manually accept rewards ensures they know what they received." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design))
- "Contextual tutorials personalize instruction, which maximizes their relevance and utility within the broader onboarding funnel." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques))
- "For these reasons, single-player games often find it harder to build and retain an audience on Roblox." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox))
- "While stunning visuals can help your game make a good first impression, the most important thing is to make sure it is fun to play." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox))
- "In incremental games, the player's primary enemy is friction (the time between upgrades)." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim))
- "Permanent rewards create pay-to-win perception and damage competitive balance." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports))
- "Educational game designers start with learning standards and objectives, then create gameplay that supports the learning." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings))
- "Roblox players like to get to the action immediately and figure it out on their own (or with friends)." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies))
- "The safest solution to safeguard an experience's economy is to have an event generate a unique event currency that is only used to purchase items from a special event shop." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/balance-virtual-economies))
- "We initially used Highlight for every object a player could interact with, but the visual noise was overwhelming." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative))
- "Conventions become established when a particular implementation or design choice grows in popularity due to its ease of use." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design))
- "we try to have like an iconic like silhouette and design and gameplay like functionality for each weapon." ([Roblox Creators 2026-10 28:13](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=1693))
- "Pallets are a scarce and limited resource to stun the killer and escape the chase, use them wisely." ([Roblox public API 2026-11 ](https://www.roblox.com/games/93978595733734/))

## [[first-session-onboarding]]
- "if you play a game you're not gonna buy something 30 seconds after joining the game" ([TheDevKing 2020-05 10:08](https://youtu.be/Zqz_gclwM0M?t=608))
- "Show don't tell. Any opportunity you have to show them what they're supposed to be doing is better than telling them." ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 32:27](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1947))
- "Roblox players form their impression very fast. It's very easy to jump into a new experience and drop out of it." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 23:37](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1417))
- "Design something that would work all on its own with players by themselves but gets better with more people." ([Last Level Studios 2021-12 22:55](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=1375))
- "an older audience is able to make do with less whereas a younger audience needs more explanation and description before they can hop into something" ([Roblox Creators 2022-11 11:02](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=662))
- "Tutorials should be a completely opt-in thing players do not like it when you spam their screen with tool tips." ([Roblox Creators 2022-12 20:31](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=1231))
- "The FTUE is arguably the single most important piece of content in a free to play game." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 1:28](https://youtu.be/XfxbdKtSbOI?t=88))
- "Regardless of how fun the gameplay may be, UI can make or break a game." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 1:10](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=70))
- "There are a lot of fun fonts out there that are hard to read and it doesn't matter how well they match the theme of your game if they're illegible." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 9:01](https://youtu.be/lOOvOIJoMi4?t=541))
- "we found this actually didn't increase our progression metrics and we were kind of confused" ([Roblox 2023-09 4:28](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=268))
- "Telling players where to go isn't quite enough; you need to equip them with how to play and why to return." ([Roblox 2023-09 11:07](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=667))
- "It's best just to reduce the amount of time people are in the lobby as much as you can." ([SmartyRBX 2024-02 11:03](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=663))
- "Even if you don't have a nice tutorial, just instrument those basic steps; that will help you see where your drop off is." ([Roblox Creators 2024-07 16:51](https://youtu.be/NFLP-FVv834?t=1011))
- "Everything is made in a way where even a six-year-old could understand what to do." ([janisjanis01 2025-06 11:12](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=672))
- "Just make a library that has all the info the player needs where it's convenient. All I'm asking for is my time to be respected." ([Cumin 2025-11 6:55](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=415))
- "The key thing that's Roblox specific is the onboarding and first-time user experience. If someone doesn't get it and get bought in, they leave." ([Ventures 2025-12 9:54](https://youtu.be/Nb50UhXcpRM?t=594))
- "You need to keep them entertained second by second to get them in that loop." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 14:00](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=840))
- "The game doesn't make you work to understand it. It just lets you play." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 1:06](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=66))
- "Dude, I can't tell you how many devs haven't even played their own game on a phone or a tablet." ([Tizzy 2026-06 15:02](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=902))
- "Text-based tutorials are awful, my opinion. There's so much research showing that text-based tutorials, kids don't read it." ([Tizzy 2026-07 14:19](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=859))
- "I didn't want my players to get bored and quit, so I decided to make the start of my game way easier and more rewarding." ([Raul 2026-07 3:27](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=207))
- "The difference between a 1k CCU game and a game that's top 100 is just relentless obsession with user experience." ([Tizzy 2026-07 11:45](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=705))
- "You don't want to just tell the player what to do. You need to give them time to understand why they should care about the actions." ([Tizzy 2026-07 4:15](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=255))
- "Onboarding is so important. Not just telling the players what to do, show them." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 4:47](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=287))
- "Players spend all their effort just trying to figure out what they actually need to do in your game." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 5:29](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=329))
- "I want this game to be super simple, like anyone should be able to play. And I don't want you to have to be doing math." ([Leif 2026-09 9:27](https://youtu.be/xVtvDYvUTqc?t=567))
- "The best games didn't just tell you what to do. They told you why you are doing it." ([Lnor Roblox 2026-09 5:00](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=300))
- "Eight players quit in the first step of the tutorial because they spawned facing away from their free egg." ([MonarchDev 2026-10 9:19](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=559))
- "This is probably the most crucial part of any single game is your onboarding." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 2:03](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=123))
- "A new player should be grouped into servers with other newer players." ([Roblox 2026-10 21:36](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=1296))
- "Think about it like a red carpet experience... you should know where to go and feel really good about it." ([Roblox 2026-10 19:13](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1153))
- "For the first 90 seconds, you want to create a frictionless experience." ([Tizzy 2026-10 2:52](https://youtu.be/_JyiKfNNqls?t=172))
- "Loading, tutorial ready, and then this is when they set a table, so 90%. That's pretty good. And then drawing submitted, that big drop." ([Rapstrike GFX 2026-10 2:42](https://youtu.be/6EoiHRT4dAA?t=162))
- "Players generally don't like to read instructions and skip through text prompts to get to gameplay." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques))
- "Lengthy, detailed tutorials are liable to bore users and cause them to bounce off your game in search of something more immediately engaging." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox))
- "Poorly designed UI can leave players confused and frustrated and lead to poor retention, while expertly designed UI can contribute to engagement and monetization." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/ui-ux-design))

## [[retention-d1-d7]]
- "Our day 7 retention was insanely low, with the last reported month with a good amount of data to analyze being May, where it was 2.68%." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/tips-for-increasing-revenue-and-maintaining-retention/329833))
- "You want them coming back to the game every single day, hopefully multiple times a week." ([Last Level Studios 2019-09 45:52](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2752))
- "Engaged players are going to come back more often... they're even more likely to spend money in your game." ([TheDevKing 2020-05 1:09](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=69))
- "Give the players a feeling to come back. Don't make it daily rewards cheap, give them a mystery they want to solve." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534))
- "you don't want them to just leave and never come back to your game" ([GnomeCode 2021-06 9:53](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=593))
- "if your experience is not fun within the first five minutes a player is going to be frustrated quit and is exponentially unlikely to come back to your game" ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 16:25](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=985))
- "Once upon a time, I worked on a very successful game... the decision was made to significantly increase the difficulty of our quests." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 11:14](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=674))
- "if all you have are long-term goals that take a long time to reach and no mid-term goals... players are going to feel like they aren't making any progress and they'll lose motivation" ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 7:19](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=439))
- "if people are playing with their friends or they're playing socially you'll generally see about 30 better retention from those users than users playing alone" ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 15:14](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=914))
- "If your experience just launched, you may want to focus on onboarding and retention instead of acquiring a large number of new players." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794))
- "getting people who kind of in their head already have their first impression of your game and have decided that they don't want to play it anymore" ([Roblox Creators 2022-06 1:27](https://youtu.be/G08BEZWLj90?t=87))
- "make a game that can hold players for a long period of time that keeps players coming back" ([ItsZyonic 2023-06 4:38](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=278))
- "The day one retention metric tells you how many players start playing your game one day and then return the next." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 2:47](https://youtu.be/XfxbdKtSbOI?t=167))
- "Roblox recommends your game to more players if it has a good play time; the longer a player stays the more chances to get recommended." ([janisjanis01 2023-08 1:20](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=80))
- "make a fun game that players want to come back to help them invest in your game and then attract new players in roughly that priority order" ([Roblox 2023-09 2:27](https://youtu.be/9eaev-ypgWo?t=147))
- "Retention drives discovery." ([Roblox 2023-09 9:31](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=571))
- "People do not like to walk around long distances, they do not like to wait for intermissions." ([SmartyRBX 2024-02 7:41](https://youtu.be/kUNFgrvUkj4?t=461))
- "whenever someone stays long enough in your game the longer they play the game the more Robux you will generate daily" ([VuukStudios 2024-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/rbVG7YBwY2k?t=0))
- "in the past 7 Days the average session time has tripled the retention is going crazy" ([janisjanis01 2024-11 0:00](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=0))
- "The fear of missing something is stronger than the desire to play." ([AlvinBlox 2025-06 6:09](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=369))
- "The fear of missing something is stronger than the desire to play." ([AlvinBlox 2025-06 6:09](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=369))
- "If you die, then you don't respawn for the rest of the level. I already hate games where I have to wait between rounds when I die." ([Cumin 2025-06 2:35](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=155))
- "A declining DAU/MAU suggests new players aren't forming daily habits with your experience." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-track-new-user-retention-by-daily-and-weekly-cohort/3817030))
- "It's actually fun to come back in two weeks and you're not under pressure to come back every day." ([Roblox 2025-09 32:12](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=1932))
- "It's honestly the number one reason that a lot of these games fall off is just your retention is not that good." ([bubDEV 2025-10 5:51](https://youtu.be/N-u2gI4DUyw?t=351))
- "My last Roblox game didn't do very well. I was really scared it was going to end up like the last game, but then the players just kept flooding into it." ([Roblox 2025-12 2:45](https://youtu.be/oDIpSDOYfY0?t=165))
- "When the ad ended, so did the player count. I lost every player. The game is dead." ([Poke 2025-12 12:27](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=747))
- "If a game can't hook me in 5 hours when I can complete other games from start to finish in 5 hours, then I just lose interest completely." ([Cumin 2025-12 16:45](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1005))
- "Regardless of whether you AFK or not or if you just leave the game, your garden is going to continue to grow." ([AlvinBlox 2026-01 5:58](https://youtu.be/BjyJlN1npdw?t=358))
- "Suddenly, you feel like you're going to miss out if you don't buy them." ([AlvinBlox 2026-01 3:56](https://youtu.be/BjyJlN1npdw?t=236))
- "Roblox loves retention. If it's high, your game is moments away from blowing up." ([janisjanis01 2026-02 12:07](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=727))
- "Fear of missing out is often stronger than intrinsic motivation to play." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606))
- "Over 10% day one retention. That's good." ([janisjanis01 2026-05 11:07](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=667))
- "Adding systems like that can add up, but they won't save your 15th percentile retention statistic. That's a core loop issue." ([Yann 2026-05 6:48](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=408))
- "Every single time a player closes the game, they know they're going to come back to more money waiting for them." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 3:09](https://youtu.be/2rLYGu0v490?t=189))
- "It is not hurting the first few days; it's actually going to still grow, but it gets to its full potential once we know more." ([Roblox Creators 2026-06 12:29](https://youtu.be/B3R1ybI3yzs?t=749))
- "The algorithm loves a retention. ... If you speak its language, it's going to be chill." ([rasdev 2026-07 2:02](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=122))
- "la vera sfida è con la retention, qui è dove si vince o si perde con i propri giochi (the real challenge is retention; that's where you win or lose)" ([Roblox Creators 2026-07 33:47](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=2027))
- "Every day a player doesn't log on, there's a chance they find a different game and never return." ([janisjanis01 2026-08 9:32](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=572))
- "make sure to rebirth as soon as possible if you want to be as strong as you can be in this game" ([Mulch 2026-08 4:00](https://youtu.be/MMuXKVreLj0?t=240))
- "Retention is king. It doesn't matter how garbage your game is, if your game has high retention, it's going to do good on the algorithm." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 5:21](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=321))
- "people play it for about 30 minutes... the problem is they never really return." ([MonarchDev 2026-08 1:22](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=82))
- "Do not give up. Because guys, if I gave up, if I did not update the game again or anything, you are not going to be seeing this." ([kra_DeZ 2026-09 12:30](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=750))
- "Nobody was ever going to find them and the people who did find them had no actual reason to return or stay past the 5-minute mark." ([MonarchDev 2026-09 3:24](https://youtu.be/LD7LGP10dRc?t=204))
- "It's all about longevity. The longer you can have a game alive on the platform the better." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 14:31](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=871))
- "my new user for session retention is actually I think the best metric so far in my game" ([Seibo 2026-10 2:04](https://youtu.be/Fh0Xku6NwOQ?t=124))
- "Every time someone joins they can do something different. Every new join equals a new experience." ([VuukStudios 2026-10 8:39](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=519))
- "The number one predictor of users coming back to the platform is a meaningful play session with a friend." ([Roblox 2026-10 2:53](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=173))
- "If you don't remember anything, retention matters the most, and then monetization." ([Roblox 2026-10 9:06](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=546))
- "Don't let them get double power." ([Roblox 2026-10 27:13](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1633))
- "if people play your game one time and they don't come back, that's going to hurt you in the long run more than it will help you" ([rasdev 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=40))
- "Anytime you can bring back a lapsed player, they're typically above the median on almost all of the metrics, especially paying." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 36:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2192))
- "Dailies can provide a reliable source of soft currency and incentivize players to log in every day." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/introduction-to-quest-design))

## [[monetisation-gamepasses]]
- "If a player purchases something that removes the challenge in the game for them, they will no longer be engaged, and also eventually leave." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902))
- "there's always a free path through so it's never a solid wall in your way that you have to pay through" ([Last Level Studios 2019-10 26:13](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1573))
- "The games that I didn't go too far one way or the other people just don't play them because they have a wealth of other content to play on the platform." ([GameMakers 2020-03 12:18](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=738))
- "Make sure you're not making your game pay to win; nobody likes that, so they won't invest into your game." ([The Letus Garden 2020-11 3:35](https://youtu.be/pAfbzNESkYk?t=215))
- "The best way to go is really just pay to skip, the most effective and most well received by both paying and non-paying players." ([GameMakers 2021-01 47:55](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2875))
- "a player who spends once in your game is exponentially more likely to spend again in the future" ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 23:13](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1393))
- "I think sometimes cosmetics is probably the best way to go with monetization." ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 1:10:52](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4252))
- "every time your block hits the collector that's value it's worth will be multiplied by two... it just applies globally to your game all the time" ([B Ricey 2022-04 1:34](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=94))
- "an anti-pay to win system is really huge for us... give everybody an equal chance to be proficient in the game without really needing to buy anything" ([Roblox Creators 2022-11 0:43](https://youtu.be/2kUFhwfuZ-w?t=43))
- "we give everybody an equal chance to be proficient in the game without really needing to buy anything" ([Roblox Creators 2022-11 38:13](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2293))
- "an older player will see this item get dunked on by it and go why am I spending time on this" ([Roblox Creators 2022-11 39:37](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2377))
- "anything that confers a gameplay advantage I believe will be perceived by the audience as pay to win and ultimately it's their perception that matters" ([GameMakers 2022-11 25:03](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=1503))
- "Game passes are great for one-time purchases; it goes into your account every time you play the game." ([SimTek Game Development 2023-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=0))
- "if you throw in monetization in there and you add that as a goal but you also make a fun game you will have the funds" ([SmartyRBX 2023-10 2:08](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=128))
- "if you are just progressing through the game it doesn't make a difference whether you've got rainbow princess dragon or rainbow Knight beagles" ([Mulch 2023-12 8:21](https://youtu.be/JwkTY1OqOjg?t=501))
- "he went like overboard with all the game passes and stuff" ([ItsZyonic 2024-03 5:50](https://youtu.be/INsV8HkDHIA?t=350))
- "When you introduce a reward for an obby in a tycoon, it becomes a cash method that cannot be automated." ([ByteBlox 2024-09 47:50](https://youtu.be/Nzrtg8SGOY0?t=2870))
- "This game pass is crazy... it picks your best rebirth button and once you have enough cash it instantly rebirths for you" ([janisjanis01 2024-11 12:58](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=778))
- "Allowing players to get game passes for free might hurt my game statistics and chances of going viral, but it's gotten to the point where I don't care." ([janisjanis01 2024-12 18:41](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=1121))
- "It's a different thing entirely when you take a significant part of the game that used to be free and then lock it behind the paywall." ([Cumin 2025-05 16:13](https://youtu.be/2auVezIcNAE?t=973))
- "Monetization, when done properly, adds to the player experience by allowing those who wish to expand it to pay more to do so." ([SmartyRBX 2025-06 37:19](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=2239))
- "I thought I monetized the game to a good amount where it wasn't too much in your face... I guess it needs to be more in your face." ([Mulch 2025-06 2:45](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=165))
- "as long as it's a fun game, it's going to be recommended" ([Roblox 2025-09 30:05](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1805))
- "Roblox kiddos are spending most of their cash on FPS skins Call of Duty-style." ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026))
- "Never lock core gameplay behind Robux cuz that's going to kill your game." ([rasdev 2026-04 3:22](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=202))
- "the pay to win packs are the largest because, bro, when kids get on Roblox, they want to feel a form of superiority over others." ([Alex Builds Games 2026-06 1:57](https://youtu.be/x_SflcuSi1w?t=117))
- "You only need like two or five of these depending on the game." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 5:13](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=313))
- "A game pass is a one-time purchase. Someone buys it once, and that's it. They never need to buy it again." ([MoseyTube 2026-07 0:50](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=50))
- "You have agency over what goes in the barrel; the risk is the blast radius when the community explodes." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 44:02](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2642))
- "A totally original, high-quality idea will not blow up if it doesn't make money." ([rasdev 2026-08 5:19](https://youtu.be/17DGro3FUlM?t=319))
- "Create a problem and then sell the solution. That is the most important thing in monetization that people don't understand." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 6:21](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=381))
- "It's pretty stupid. Doesn't really make sense, but here we are." ([revolver agz 2026-09 2:07](https://youtu.be/j3L0K1yZjms?t=127))
- "Monetization, which is pretty horrible in my opinion. 0.6%. I'm aiming for 2% for this." ([Seibo 2026-09 5:35](https://youtu.be/NqW98hvXUhc?t=335))
- "Free passholders should not be able to complete premium missions or earn premium tier rewards unless they upgrade to the premium pass." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design))
- "Using this strategy for every possible complementary item might distract from players' browsing experience and from their immersion in the environment." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/contextual-purchases))
- "Having both free and premium passes ensures that non-paying players can still earn rewards and enjoy your experience, while rewarding paying players with worthwhile bonuses." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/monetization-foundations))

## [[monetisation-devproducts]]
- "A developer product can be bought multiple times, so it's a great way to monetize your game along with game passes." ([AlvinBlox 2018-08 25:18](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=1518))
- "Hard currencies must be carefully designed so that they remain rare." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/monetisation-101-how-to-effectively-generate-revenue-in-your-roblox-game/246902))
- "players that have purchased something in a game are more likely to purchase something again and that is a fact" ([TheDevKing 2020-05 8:33](https://youtu.be/Zqz_gclwM0M?t=513))
- "If your Dev Product does not satisfy the player within the first minute, you most likely failed." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-game-monetization-guide/1235207))
- "Whenever we do sales maybe like a 25, 30% sell off, we see a huge spike, two, three, four times increase in revenue." ([Roblox Creators 2023-01 0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0))
- "for some reason you can only go back a couple months on developer products" ([Mulch 2023-04 5:30](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=330))
- "it has no game passes it just has developer products which they are kind of broken a little bit" ([PolarBear 2024-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/Q-Wbi1an-ww?t=0))
- "We ended up adding this starter pack for 79 Robux... And as soon as we added this into the game, the game started making two to three times more money." ([1Coal 2024-03 2:25](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=145))
- "a developer product can be purchased over and over again unlike a Game Pass which can only be purchased once" ([AlvinBlox 2024-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=0))
- "they're all at spawn so you're immediately aware of these things which is what makes them purchased so many times" ([CallMeDogey 2025-01 10:14](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=614))
- "Super Pack, Starter Pack, and Grower Flowers are the top three." ([Quez 2025-06 1:58](https://youtu.be/WOG3Rttf-V4?t=118))
- "I've spent well over 10,000 Robux. What's the point?" ([Bemmy 2025-06 9:49](https://youtu.be/-Z0C5YJtnqY?t=589))
- "all the like dev products are really, really cheap" ([Arow 2025-07 1:09](https://youtu.be/KsaRtStNO3s?t=69))
- "You certainly shouldn't think every single ad is stealing someone away who was going to buy it." ([Roblox Creators 2025-08 22:35](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1355))
- "Basically nothing. Right now, if you want this to be better, you'll probably want to do 2x money so that it's more enticing to the player." ([PineappleDev 2025-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/gXKoxaK6Ufg?t=0))
- "In my experience, it's pretty difficult to actually monetize Roblox games." ([PineappleDev 2025-09 2:30](https://youtu.be/gXKoxaK6Ufg?t=150))
- "Products should feel like a must-have, not a nice-to-have... they should be solving a pain." ([Tizzy 2026-06 14:12](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=852))
- "Consumables really pump this stat because they can buy the 24-hour lock today, tomorrow, the next day." ([Tizzy 2026-06 5:24](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=324))
- "Sell other things instead of currency packs cuz I'm not going to lie, that's just mid." ([Alex Builds Games 2026-06 4:24](https://youtu.be/x_SflcuSi1w?t=264))
- "Your best-selling products are always going to be the ones that solve a specific pain that your players are already feeling, not features that are nice to have." ([MoseyTube 2026-07 1:51](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=111))
- "Right now I'm spending more than I'm truly making." ([MonarchDev 2026-08 4:47](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=287))
- "You create the worth yourself. The player has to think they're getting the better end of the deal." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 6:47](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=407))
- "Dev products is like items that you can buy anytime. But game passes are items that you can literally only buy once." ([kra_DeZ 2026-09 13:51](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=831))
- "Robux purchase conversion rate is 1%... 7,000 players saw the Robux prompt, but only a 100 bought it" ([Seibo 2026-10 3:36](https://youtu.be/Fh0Xku6NwOQ?t=216))
- "Dev Products generally monetize better, as they can be repeatedly purchased." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization))
- "Do not use the PromptProductPurchaseFinished event to process purchases. You must use the ProcessReceipt callback instead." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products))
- "The functions for handling each product ID must return true for the transaction to be successful." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products))

## [[monetisation-other]]
- "when kids play your game there are millions of other games they can play; if they can't enjoy your game they will click out" ([Roblox 2018-07 46:58](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=2818))
- "Payouts are awarded consistently to all developers, regardless of scale of their player base: a unit of Premium time spent is rewarded with the same amount of R$." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-premium-payouts/455347))
- "I don't even need game passes or any donation buttons at all when really you can actually make quite a lot of money off of premium payouts." ([ThatGuyEvan 2020-02 1:22](https://youtu.be/PHi1aaPhDzg?t=82))
- "don't make it pay to win, don't give you no more beneficial things... avoid paywalls" ([AlvinBlox 2020-03 3:42](https://youtu.be/oh89BQkBvBM?t=222))
- "Monetization is usually an augmentation just a base game so if you don't have a solid foundation it's just going to be a multiple awful small amount." ([GameMakers 2020-03 19:29](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=1169))
- "Premium Payouts are the only way to currently make money even when you don't have products for sale." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-game-monetization-guide/1235207))
- "Content is your most valuable resource in your game. You put a lot of effort into creating it and if players run out, they might get bored and leave." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 26:43](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1603))
- "I recommend steering clear of tasks that require hard currency spend." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 8:32](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=512))
- "You give them things to spend it on that they get to keep, but forcing them to spend it... that's just going to be a really frustrating blocker." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 34:44](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=2084))
- "you want players to be spending out of fun not out of necessity" ([Roblox Creators 2022-11 47:50](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2870))
- "if you're building a game that has less monetization or less egregious monetization you're gonna need that conversion to go up like dramatically" ([GameMakers 2022-11 38:37](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2317))
- "if it was just the regular currency then players who have been playing the game... just go buy everything out on day one" ([Roblox Creators 2023-01 1:32](https://youtu.be/okAygI1sxsA?t=92))
- "Make it a fun feature from the start, design to be fun and valuable to the players from the start. Then later think whether monetization fits." ([Roblox Creators 2023-01 0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0))
- "Definitely don't do stock. I would not recommend." ([Roblox Creators 2023-01 19:12](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=1152))
- "Make sure you rectify for those users." ([Roblox Creators 2023-06 0:07](https://youtu.be/yn4V-fBdUm8?t=7))
- "We've heard of some of you guys hacking Subs with Dev products today, and then we've heard from some of you who would just prefer to monetize via subscriptions." ([Roblox 2023-09 18:17](https://youtu.be/8R136Y_T5G4?t=1097))
- "for users who are teleporting out of your experience you're actually earning more from the immersive ads earnings than you would from those users in your experience" ([Roblox 2023-09 24:20](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1460))
- "Subscriptions are charged in real-world currency to support automatic renewals across platforms, which enhances subscriber retention and creates ongoing revenue opportunities for creators." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-and-manage-subscriptions-within-your-experience/2600664))
- "while 98% of players won't spend much money, the remaining 2% are willing to pay so much, that they could make 90% of the revenue of a game." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921))
- "those numbers could be off I'm just using the numbers that I have they could have earned more or less because it all depends on how much premium players actually play" ([PineappleDev 2024-01 1:37](https://youtu.be/-LXvkK5YkNM?t=97))
- "Subscriptions can only be prompted, so the speaker wants a subscriptions button in the menu." ([ByteBlox 2024-04 8:04](https://youtu.be/7NRp4BEYrsQ?t=484))
- "Race to the end and show your friends who's the parkour Obby master." ([Mulch 2024-06 5:34](https://youtu.be/pqCXX06vlDw?t=334))
- "from my own experience I think you're going to get like 50 Robux because of premium payouts" ([TheDankDeveloper 2024-07 6:54](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=414))
- "We take a rake and we feel we have to because without taking a rake a lot more currency stays in the system and drives inflation." ([GameMakers 2024-08 50:34](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3034))
- "console users have spent 2x more per hour than mobile users" ([Roblox 2024-09 5:00](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=300))
- "you do not want your players to be overly Rich" ([Roblox 2024-09 19:30](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1170))
- "when you sell assets you could be lying in bed and an order could come in at 2 a.m." ([SmartyRBX 2024-09 4:40](https://youtu.be/59AbBgxhfTA?t=280))
- "they don't try and take away a lot of your money they want to make a fun experience and then they're like okay then we can monetize it" ([ByteBlox 2024-11 1:29](https://youtu.be/Jl0SdXksQsI?t=89))
- "Engagement-based payouts let you earn Robux based on how much time Premium members engage in an experience." ([Roblox DevForum 2024-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/paid-access-experiences-will-be-eligible-for-engagement-based-payouts/3275498))
- "The game knows that I'm already a pretty loyal player... so what the game is going to do is lower the chance of me getting a good pet." ([ByteBlox 2025-02 16:44](https://youtu.be/o-8V99horro?t=1004))
- "our total Robux made was a whopping 21 Robux yep that's all we made" ([JustDucksey 2025-02 8:24](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=504))
- "Creator Rewards is a bonus program that lets creators who publish experiences earn directly from the engagement their content drives on the platform." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-creator-rewards-earn-more-by-growing-the-community/3777628))
- "Creators own the implementation within their game, including reward prompt, frequency, reward value, and reward type." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678))
- "I feel like it's better for smaller games that don't really make as much, you know, just giving those developers a little bit extra that they need." ([PineappleDev 2025-07 3:22](https://youtu.be/zaQltaqOV7I?t=202))
- "It can just change a lot based on all your monetization strategies." ([PineappleDev 2025-08 3:35](https://youtu.be/tsmsTtrlT8Y?t=215))
- "have that in early because one, you never know when you can convert on somebody" ([Roblox 2025-09 23:17](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=1397))
- "These Roblox devs now implement every single monetization strategy in the book." ([Cumin 2025-11 6:13](https://youtu.be/66n0P2NtBoQ?t=373))
- "Energy Incremental, I made it a point to not go too crazy on the monetization, and I think that's why it earned so little." ([1_Bruce 2025-12 15:37](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=937))
- "Average bookings per paying user actually fell 13% year-over-year, as the flood of new international users diluted per-user economics." ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-roblox-still-growing-in-2026-a-data-driven-look-at-the-numbers))
- "Creators have used developer products and passes to facilitate Robux donations and tipping in games. However, these tools were never designed for peer-to-peer Robux transfers." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/disabling-cross-game-sales-of-passes-and-dev-products-and-introducing-the-transfers-api/4618396))
- "For each transfer, Roblox doesn't take a platform fee. Creators using the API earn a 10% commission and recipients receive 90%." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/disabling-cross-game-sales-of-passes-and-dev-products-and-introducing-the-transfers-api/4618396))
- "If users actually have to want to engage with this on their own. You shouldn't force them to engage with it." ([Roblox Creators 2026-05 3:16](https://youtu.be/R6HbXwyuHzY?t=196))
- "I took the same exact formula, changed the theme, and got a viral game." ([janisjanis01 2026-06 12:51](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=771))
- "our monetization system is fundamentally strong and will compound over time with additional levers, including subscription and advertising revenues." ([Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07 ](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript))
- "Making 137 Robux after spending over 50,000 is super demotivating." ([Raul 2026-08 6:13](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=373))
- "Create a problem and sell the solution." ([MoseyTube 2026-08 4:26](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=266))
- "Turn on the in-game shop button via Creator Hub > Monetization > Shop to maximize visibility." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-provided-discounts-on-in-game-items/4861443))
- "This means that you can continue to play, test, and publish without having to 'buy back in' at the retail Robux rate." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505))
- "Roblox Card will be a card connected to a Roblox Wallet. Creators can only spend what is available in their Wallet balance. This is not a credit card." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505))
- "If your game is buns, you're not making no money." ([rasdev 2026-09 4:46](https://youtu.be/Df5ebFGEOXY?t=286))
- "Makeup's been a very big driver in my overall growth this year as a marketplace creator." ([Roblox 2026-10 13:34](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=814))
- "Never interrupt a fight. Only prompt at natural breaks: the lobby, death screen, or results screen." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/shooter))
- "Rewards should be valuable enough for players to watch ads to unlock them, but should not be tagged to the highest revenue item in your game." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/shooter))
- "Economic location is not always the same as account location. To determine a user's economic location, Roblox looks at signals, including VPN usage, billing history, and account history." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing))
- "Contextual purchases are designed opportunistically, operating under a "right item, right time" framework." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/contextual-purchases))
- "Monetization features are some of the most prominent content interaction spaces and directly impacts an experience's monetary growth potential." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/monetization-foundations))
- "Per Roblox 2026 standards, no RNG. You cannot reward a "random item." State exactly what the player receives: "Watch to receive [Specific Item]."" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival))
- "Rewards should be items that are used up and reset, not permanent gear, tools, or stat upgrades. Scarcity is the core loop of survival games." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival))
- "Players are highly motivated to watch ads if it means accelerating their progress." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon))
- "if you offer paid random items that users can purchase with Robux or other in-game currency purchasable with Robux, you must indicate all possible outcomes and the actual numerical odds of what they may receive." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-random-items))

## [[pricing]]
- "there is no right answer to that question and it's I think the only way you can recover from it is to say that pricing is going to be something that we change" ([Roblox 2018-07 26:51](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1611))
- "Usually their core and only KPI is how much money they can bring in in a given year." ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 33:29](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=2009))
- "we want those to kind of represent the Rarity through their price" ([Roblox Creators 2023-01 2:54](https://youtu.be/okAygI1sxsA?t=174))
- "helping them make that decision by doing the math for them" ([Roblox Creators 2023-01 5:50](https://youtu.be/okAygI1sxsA?t=350))
- "Don't be ashamed of putting on those really low numbers, especially if it's a lower amount of currency" ([Roblox Creators 2023-03 0:07](https://youtu.be/Cli3ogW-azk?t=7))
- "Only show offers and only offer Dev products game passes to our players when they're good value." ([Roblox 2023-09 19:14](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=1154))
- "if you make it 200 then it just doesn't look appealing... 199 that looks like eh I can probably spend that money" ([PolarBear 2024-02 2:02](https://youtu.be/nkmfdUbj4bQ?t=122))
- "Anchor pricing... prompt them with something that is very expensive or above the average price... then when they see a lower price and it's a really good deal, they will want to spend that money." ([Stewiepfing 2024-02 2:17](https://youtu.be/v8tFfBTr6M8?t=137))
- "I was absolutely blown away by this like bro it's actually like decent like good quality" ([ItsZyonic 2024-03 5:08](https://youtu.be/INsV8HkDHIA?t=308))
- "If I do, it will cancel everybody's private server subscription." ([Mulch 2024-06 4:10](https://youtu.be/pqCXX06vlDw?t=250))
- "it had to be just a no-brainer if you are spending on Roblox... it has to be the best offering" ([Roblox 2024-09 26:39](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1599))
- "we did the 12-week trial of price optimization and I am a huge evangelist for it now" ([Roblox 2024-09 31:23](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1883))
- "We spend a lot of time doing market research... we look at comparables we look at our own catalog like what is performing" ([Roblox 2024-09 28:00](https://youtu.be/NEK7jq1LFu8?t=1680))
- "There's an optimal price point for each item in different regions of the world." ([Roblox 2024-09 12:25](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=745))
- "My game could be balanced a little better, so I'm going to buff the pet multipliers by 30%." ([janisjanis01 2024-12 9:20](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=560))
- "I think this should not be a 10,000 Robux unit." ([Bemmy 2025-06 10:29](https://youtu.be/-Z0C5YJtnqY?t=629))
- "I'm almost always pricing at the price floor, because everybody's comparing themselves." ([Roblox Creators 2025-08 11:52](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=712))
- "Brand new Roblox games are typically valued a lot lower than a game that is established." ([Roblox 2025-09 13:38](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=818))
- "I price our top products based off of Robux bundles." ([1_Bruce 2026-03 4:05](https://youtu.be/zgq6L4ZzCHs?t=245))
- "We decided to set our prices extremely cheap just to boost like payer conversion rate, but honestly, during the peak of the game we probably could have doubled all these prices" ([Yann 2026-05 2:44](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=164))
- "You don't want to just make it like one bill money is like nine Robux. Like, that's going to cook you." ([Tizzy 2026-06 12:09](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=729))
- "give them the option to reroll for cheaper for like four or five Robux, and they keep rerolling" ([Alex Builds Games 2026-06 14:48](https://youtu.be/x_SflcuSi1w?t=888))
- "If you lock content behind day one DLC or special editions of the game, that means that was already done by the game's release." ([Cumin 2026-06 9:10](https://youtu.be/KNKxJ06w1Uo?t=550))
- "It's all about the timing and placement in the psychological levers that you're pulling at the exact right time." ([Tizzy 2026-07 17:26](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=1046))
- "GTA can price whatever they want. Like this could have easily been 100, and would still sell absolutely well." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 3:33](https://youtu.be/i6F_0YVzhz8?t=213))
- "I think a lot of people are out there basically bitching because they left money on the table by not having a $100 SKU." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 5:35](https://youtu.be/i6F_0YVzhz8?t=335))
- "These offers are provided entirely by Roblox. Creators will continue to receive the normal payout based on the item's undiscounted price." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-provided-discounts-on-in-game-items/4861443))
- "We cut the prices of every game pass and dev product by half. What happened? Our revenue doubled." ([VuukStudios 2026-10 3:42](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=222))
- "Higher prices does not mean better revenue." ([VuukStudios 2026-10 5:01](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=301))
- "Time is the big variable we use. We price what an hour of their time is worth to a paying user." ([Roblox 2026-10 13:11](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=791))
- "A discount is not genuine if an item is always 'on sale' for the same amount of Robux." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization))

## [[ads-and-sponsored-experiences]]
- "Just taking a minute to invest some Robux into ads consistently, you'll definitely see a result and make more money." ([Roblox 2018-07 5:11](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=311))
- "advertisements that don't meet the minimum threshold will almost always have horrible return." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805))
- "you'll probably be able to generate something in Paint .Net or Photoshop in less then an hour that will receive a higher CTR." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805))
- "We never had to spend a dollar on advertising for that game and we launched with 70 000 users." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 24:46](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=1486))
- "in 10 minutes time the game won't be getting as much promotion so the robux sales are probably gonna go down by quite a bit" ([Mulch 2021-09 9:19](https://youtu.be/yG44d_Xnbwg?t=559))
- "The daily budget serves as the budget for the day but also as the equivalent of a bid." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 25:55](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1555))
- "There is an opportunity to very efficiently leverage the sponsored ads platform on Roblox to drive discovery." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 22:13](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1333))
- "85 to 95 of new creative concepts fail to outperform the best ad in an existing portfolio" ([GameMakers 2022-03 1:26](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=86))
- "in facebook you cannot remove history of the ad spend from the winning ad therefore if you try to combine a challenger into the campaign with the champion the challenger almost always loses" ([GameMakers 2022-03 33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032))
- "You're borrowing or renting really the awareness and affinity that a brand has built over decades." ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 12:12](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=732))
- "I think it's the recent change to kind of put those all in one sort versus like scatter them across the website through all the sorts" ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 0:47](https://youtu.be/HxqWoF_wfMQ?t=47))
- "it has really been I'd say at our assessment just a lack of big companies specifically being able to adapt quickly" ([GameMakers 2023-05 1:21](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=81))
- "clearly there's a massive impact to the whole sector right but that's very different from you know does it have to be an impact to you in your business" ([GameMakers 2023-05 2:43](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=163))
- "I wouldn't recommend banking on ads and I would recommend using sponsors over ads" ([ItsZyonic 2023-06 1:37](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=97))
- "you don't have to have a million impressions 20,000 clicks 1,000 players to earn Robux" ([PolarBear 2024-02 1:23](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=83))
- "the minimum you should put in is like 70 or 100" ([PolarBear 2024-02 4:46](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=286))
- "Make it smart. Don't just blatantly put it out there. Make it ads where the player will likely look at the ad but honestly won't even realize that it's an ad." ([ByteBlox 2024-02 4:18](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=258))
- "I spent 5,000 Robux for basically just visits I didn't even make anything back which is kind of tragic" ([PineappleDev 2024-03 4:58](https://youtu.be/NB8XNNDdSjQ?t=298))
- "if you just want to like try to get people to find your game then yes I guess it could be worth it" ([PineappleDev 2024-03 4:58](https://youtu.be/NB8XNNDdSjQ?t=298))
- "we actually did not spend all the ad tokens we spent about half of them" ([PolarBear 2024-03 3:44](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=224))
- "I would not spend under 10,000 Robux on sponsoring a game." ([PolarBear 2024-04 2:05](https://youtu.be/Hh31hjo3J6U?t=125))
- "you can sponsor top content creators to promote your game and get it into the spotlight" ([SmartyRBX 2024-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=0))
- "Me and CodeBro are both putting 2,800 Robux in cuz that's the minimum that you can do" ([ItsZyonic 2024-06 4:42](https://youtu.be/b7qEitHbhUA?t=282))
- "I spent $20 in the ad and 105 Robux isn't much so I don't really know if it's worth it" ([BloxxedDev 2024-08 1:16](https://youtu.be/fDJoaxTuRf4?t=76))
- "it seemed that I got everything when it peaked when it had lots of plays on it once" ([BloxxedDev 2024-08 1:49](https://youtu.be/fDJoaxTuRf4?t=109))
- "Social media almost never picks up, unless your game looks incredibly interesting on a 30s clip." ([Roblox DevForum 2024-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791))
- "Contacting youtubers is also usually a big waste of time in the release." ([Roblox DevForum 2024-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791))
- "users that are acquired through search ads are significantly more valuable than users that we've seen come in through sponsored experiences" ([Roblox 2024-09 6:43](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=403))
- "this is where most people get it wrong you want to actually bid like 0.01 or 0.02" ([waveshine 2024-10 4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242))
- "probably not I still think you're better off just focusing on making a good game the algorithm is pretty good if your game's good it will get picked up" ([GnomeCode 2024-11 11:05](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=665))
- "if your game has zero players the algorithm doesn't know about you right Roblox doesn't know about your game and so you can just spend some money in advertising" ([GnomeCode 2024-11 11:46](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=706))
- "you're better off finding content creators who can make a video on your game this will be the best way to gain players" ([janisjanis01 2024-12 9:29](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=569))
- "the objective was to get visits not awareness" ([JustDucksey 2025-02 0:44](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=44))
- "they say you should spend at least 0.04 to 0.25 but that isn't actually true" ([BubuTheDev 2025-03 1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82))
- "we definitely got cooked on this one... we're going to try to make it easier. We're going to add more levels so the player is engaged" ([Quez 2025-03 4:33](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=273))
- "We made 166 Robux. So, if we do the calculations here, we only lost 4,964 Robux." ([Quez 2025-03 4:00](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=240))
- "there is almost no other place that advertisers can advertise to get to this audience. So the CPMs are going to be likely a little bit higher." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-04 7:43](https://youtu.be/v_0RADBCTlw?t=463))
- "for the first week, I would do about 1,000 Robux a day, just to get your game in the algorithm" ([Arow 2025-05 2:03](https://youtu.be/_fVivJ4DtsA?t=123))
- "I spent about 4,000 Robux to advertise this game, which is not a lot at all." ([PineappleDev 2025-05 4:07](https://youtu.be/o4dJZ-UqznE?t=247))
- "Running for at least one to two weeks allows Roblox to optimize, get you the right players." ([Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880))
- "Our creators actually like need less targeting because the Roblox ads manager is really helping you find the best users." ([Roblox Creators 2025-06 36:18](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2178))
- "not only have I lost my time and labor, but 99,269 Robux." ([Mulch 2025-06 9:35](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=575))
- "Eligible creators earn Robux for every ad shown within their experience, and early tests have shown that users who view ads spend more Robux within experiences." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678))
- "We made a total of 1,200 Robux, which means we got like half of our Robux back." ([TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688))
- "50 ad credits for like 7 days... 50 ad credits is pretty standard." ([Erthlord 2025-08 7:03](https://youtu.be/dB6Us5PaWFw?t=423))
- "If you do a standalone game, you may or may not get any visits at all. You're not going to get meaningful reach from a brand game with two or ten concurrent users." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 29:14](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1754))
- "You have to have a 100,000 DAUs on your game or experience to even apply to do rewarded video." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 10:19](https://youtu.be/LRyThu3bFyg?t=619))
- "We actually found out that 99% of the times we actually see lift in total impressions" ([Roblox 2025-09 25:58](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1558))
- "Ads are not meant to fix this trend." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157))
- "As long as the experience has no free-form user creation or AI interaction, any public, unrestricted experience with over 2,000 unique monthly visitors can integrate Rewarded Video." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278))
- "Monetization from advertising is not currently factored into the Home Recommendations algorithm." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278))
- "What I learned was it takes a lot of Robux to make a game popular. I don't think 10k is enough." ([JustVeryYellow 2025-11 11:23](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=683))
- "Don't trust anyone that tells you that there's some hidden configuration that if you don't give them 50% of your game, you won't be able to set yourself." ([bubDEV 2025-12 6:12](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=372))
- "if you're advertising your own game, you don't really need to put that much in ads to start." ([ItsZyonic 2026-01 5:13](https://youtu.be/4-ge-E9WvM4?t=313))
- "If you don't have the Robux to launch ads, this method is definitely an option." ([janisjanis01 2026-01 13:03](https://youtu.be/IWzAHgHPhhU?t=783))
- "ads on Roblox are really more about exposure and building a player base, not making your money back right away." ([ThePurpleGuy 2026-02 1:21](https://youtu.be/VEkuM5ZG6G4?t=81))
- "Part of what grows LTV is also boosting your retention rates. If we're juicing our ARPDAU by decreasing our retention, that's going to make this a lot less exciting." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 7:29](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=449))
- "Ad networks are optimizing for CPM, not ARPDAU... they don't pay that retention tax. The publisher does." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 9:51](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=591))
- "You should always do daily budget. Never, ever, ever do lifetime." ([CallMeDogey 2026-03 6:50](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=410))
- "The ads are really just buying you enough data to let the algorithm take over." ([MoseyTube 2026-04 4:47](https://youtu.be/sFr7htxsnR4?t=287))
- "You genuinely do not need this much for your game to blow up." ([Duckable 2026-05 9:38](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=578))
- "No saving, no testing lightly, just full send." ([Lnor Roblox 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122))
- "if your game starts dying, just run more ads can be some of the worst advice, mostly" ([Tizzy 2026-05 12:12](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=732))
- "Once your home recommendation impressions have surpassed your ad impressions, stop ads." ([Tizzy 2026-05 15:03](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=903))
- "You put your game in front of everyone who could possibly play it, and let the algorithm that Roblox spent years optimizing decide who will play your game." ([Yann 2026-05 2:43](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=163))
- "Brandformance: the intersection of brand marketing (awareness, hard to track) and performance marketing." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 3:25](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=205))
- "Do commissions, get money for ads." ([Tizzy 2026-06 12:05](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=725))
- "Roblox has no idea if your game is good yet. So, it's just not going to push it to anybody at all, and ads are basically just how you give Roblox enough data" ([MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19))
