- "The algorithm already evaluated it once and this run is basically just showing it the updated version." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 3:16](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=196))
- "Use ads for only one thing, getting enough initial players so Roblox can have numbers on your game." ([rasdev 2026-06 3:23](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=203))
- "Ads are hands-down the fastest way to get players into your game, but they don't fix a bad game." ([DitchyDevelopments 2026-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/cfLbW2j2VLs?t=41))
- "Stop wasting money on ads to fix a game problem." ([rasdev 2026-07 2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163))
- "In hindsight, I would have paid for the highly engaged players 100,000 Robux fee, but I did not." ([Yann 2026-07 2:11](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=131))
- "We were paying for clicks that could not become plays." ([Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377))
- "If your game is good, you can drop 5k Robux and get 10k players. If your game is garbage, you can spend 2 to 5k USD and get 50 players." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 6:31](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=391))
- "Once that happens, you don't need to keep paying for ads, you can just let the algorithm carry the momentum instead." ([MoseyTube 2026-08 2:06](https://youtu.be/5zz5ao0zV_Y?t=126))
- "You just need to trust your ads. The real variable is if your game is actually good." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 2:03](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=123))
- "Let your ads run. Let Roblox do its work." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 4:06](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=246))
- "It's essentially me spending money on the endless void of what Roblox calls an ad system." ([MonarchDev 2026-08 5:02](https://youtu.be/MANBsA1MTzI?t=302))
- "Ads only scale what's already working." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 1:22](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=82))
- "Algorithm cannot pick you up by just letting your game sit." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 2:02](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=122))
- "So, I knew I had to take a risk and turn the ads off. When I turned them off, the CCU dropped." ([ItsZyonic 2026-09 5:04](https://youtu.be/RIWPfMJyVhQ?t=304))
- "Do Roblox ads... Roblox ads is king, it'll always help you." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 6:41](https://youtu.be/eS0Vc7h0VW0?t=401))
- "We started with a small amount of ads and it started raising our CCU and all of our metrics started growing again." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790))
- "It was almost a no brainer for us to keep pushing forward with ads." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790))
- "I generally lowered the ads ad credits amounts when the home recommendations number went higher than the ad credits number." ([Seibo 2026-10 0:43](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=43))
- "I spent $130 over 10 days. And obviously, I had a very high click-through rate." ([Rapstrike GFX 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/7yNr2prDUsE?t=0))
- "Just because more people saw your game does not mean more people are going to play it." ([rasdev 2026-10 10:16](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=616))
- "Rewarded video ads are fully opt-in, they don't interrupt their experience; players stay in control." ([Roblox 2026-10 10:19](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=619))
- "It doesn't matter if your game is buns, you're not going to get anywhere. So, this is only for games that are already pretty good." ([rasdev 2026-10 2:17](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=137))
- "the Roblox ad algorithm is learning who wants to play this type of game. And then it's going to start pushing out to like my real target audience." ([tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868))
- "Eligibility does not guarantee Roblox will serve ads in your game, and nothing obligates Roblox to serve ads into your game." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads))
- "weave the brand into your game's core loop" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization))
- "Qualified visits are billed once per session; engaging 10 branded assets in one session counts as 1 qualified visit." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations))
- "Campaign eligibility is determined by the most restrictive asset label applied to any of your registered assets." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations))
- "Learn how to increase earnings with rewarded video and get inspiration from top games in your genre." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/rewarded-video-guides))
- "Engagement, retention, and monetization from users acquired through ads are excluded from RFY ranking signals." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq))
- "Search ads differ from other ads in that they also calculate the relevance of the game to the user's query. This means that games that are more relevant to a search query have a greater chance of displaying as ads." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads))
- "At this time, search ad and sponsored ad results are combined as a single Sponsored Ad metric on acquisition reports." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads))
- "The two metrics that you need to focus on are opt-in rate and daily frequency." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim))
- "Rewarded video should act as the edge a player reaches for right before a match, or the lifeline they grab right after a loss, never a distraction from the action." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports))
- "In an RPG, an ad is not an interruption; it is a micro-transaction paid for with time." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg))
- "Rewards for video ads cannot be randomized items." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads))
- "Issue the reward to the user immediately after the rewarded video ad has finished playing." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads))

## [[thumbnails-icons]]
- "the creative is ultimate the ultimate entity that we control less and less everything else right" ([GameMakers 2021-11 32:56](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=1976))
- "green and blue tend to have a higher click-through rate on YouTube thumbnails and Roblox icons" ([1Coal 2023-05 4:02](https://youtu.be/JzB8fpyofnw?t=242))
- "on a page where you only have a few milliseconds to convince someone to play your game, it works quite well." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921))
- "the positioning is very important because if you have bland positions people will just think oh this guy sucks let's not click on his thumbnail" ([SmartyRBX 2024-02 1:22](https://youtu.be/c94geZ19UnQ?t=82))
- "with a good camera angle a good fov good lighting it looks amazing" ([SmartyRBX 2024-02 3:24](https://youtu.be/c94geZ19UnQ?t=204))
- "I spent nearly 2 hours trying to make a game Icon here's me making like 50% of it and then I just pay the guy to do the rest" ([TheDankDeveloper 2024-04 6:07](https://youtu.be/PZ3J9H-3fv8?t=367))
- "these are easy to do... all these are so easy to do" ([PolarBear 2024-07 1:20](https://youtu.be/tkAis2wTxms?t=80))
- "Honestly I think the hardest part about making a thumbnail is probably not actually making the thumbnail, it's coming up with the idea." ([bubDEV 2024-08 0:41](https://youtu.be/th2DJ7-5MnU?t=41))
- "If you're not creative and don't know how to make one for your specific video then you're in trouble." ([bubDEV 2024-08 17:55](https://youtu.be/th2DJ7-5MnU?t=1075))
- "I've seen a lot of fantastic games that just have a horrible icon or thumbnail and they could be getting up to four times the amount of people recommended" ([Roblox 2024-09 30:53](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1853))
- "just because the thumbnail is winning right now doesn't mean it will always be winning" ([Roblox Creators 2024-11 6:59](https://youtu.be/I65eJ_uUsY8?t=419))
- "we're optimizing for qualified playthrough rate which means that users not only have to click your thumbnail and join but also they have to stick around and do something in your game" ([Roblox Creators 2024-11 7:50](https://youtu.be/I65eJ_uUsY8?t=470))
- "If your thumbnail looks like it was made in 5 seconds with no effort or thought, no one is going to click on it." ([AlvinBlox 2025-04 0:30](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=30))
- "we build it with the mindset that we need to partner with a creator. this is their baby, their experience." ([Roblox 2025-04 21:31](https://youtu.be/vTc2wciWukE?t=1291))
- "As an artist, you know how much we hate AI generated images." ([Cumin 2025-05 1:24](https://youtu.be/E7bedfC8A10?t=84))
- "Remember your audience. Thumbnail tests are typically shown on home recommendations to new players." ([Roblox 2025-09 33:46](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=2026))
- "If your clothing's trash, no one's gonna tap in." ([#7Phantom 2025-10 4:28](https://youtu.be/sByQYCWWlwE?t=268))
- "These videos will play automatically before your static thumbnails and really give users a pure glimpse into your experience before they can join." ([Roblox Creators 2025-11 0:15](https://youtu.be/ag569uHM96E?t=15))
- "Avoid any misleading edits and make sure not to have any off-platform footage." ([Roblox Creators 2025-11 1:32](https://youtu.be/ag569uHM96E?t=92))
- "You can choose up to 10 custom thumbnails to find the ads that work for you and your campaign." ([Roblox Creators 2026-02 2:17](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=137))
- "You need to learn to make your own thumbnails because hiring artists takes time." ([CallMeDogey 2026-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/SjjPGduApZU?t=0))
- "Make sure you save an image of a better-looking thumbnail you found online and ask it to recreate it." ([CallMeDogey 2026-04 2:44](https://youtu.be/SjjPGduApZU?t=164))
- "Your game is judged on one image, just one image before anyone ever plays it." ([rasdev 2026-05 4:30](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=270))
- "if people aren't clicking, you're not getting anywhere regardless of how good the game is." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 3:33](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=213))
- "the most common mistake I see is devs make a thumbnail that looks good on desktop but is very hard to see on mobile." ([Tizzy 2026-06 5:33](https://youtu.be/az7em1IoBhA?t=333))
- "As you get more impressions, your Q PTR will always go down. Almost always. So you want to keep your creative fresh." ([Tizzy 2026-06 8:01](https://youtu.be/az7em1IoBhA?t=481))
- "the thumbnail to communicate what the player can expect to feel when playing." ([Tizzy 2026-06 8:43](https://youtu.be/az7em1IoBhA?t=523))
- "I've never once hired a thumbnail artist that has beaten my CTR or PTR." ([Tizzy 2026-07 7:26](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=446))
- "If you cannot make a clickable icon before you design the game and code... then there's genuinely no point because the icon will get people to click." ([rasdev 2026-07 2:01](https://youtu.be/fZJEDcibS_M?t=121))
- "If you can't make an icon that people want to click, the game doesn't even matter." ([rasdev 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/9_cHgdOo7i0?t=0))
- "A mediocre game with a good thumbnail beats a game with a bad thumbnail that has great gameplay at first." ([rasdev 2026-07 1:22](https://youtu.be/9_cHgdOo7i0?t=82))
- "Your icon has to communicate what the player will feel when they play, not what the game is." ([rasdev 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/_Tt-l7EMEHE?t=0))
- "An icon is never good on its own. It's either good or bad compared to its competitors." ([rasdev 2026-07 2:44](https://youtu.be/_Tt-l7EMEHE?t=164))
- "Sometimes the difference between a successful game and a failed one is literally just the thumbnail." ([janisjanis01 2026-08 6:05](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=365))
- "Roblox doesn't let you just swap out of the thumbnail on an active ad." ([Raul 2026-08 4:04](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=244))
- "The video communicated the pacing of the game in a way thumbnails could not." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gameplay-videos-on-home-help-your-games-get-discovered/4842601))
- "Your icon has about half a second on their phone or on their laptop, and it has to be very instantly understood before the players read what the title of the game is about." ([rasdev 2026-09 2:42](https://youtu.be/IyEqyPOjEvU?t=162))
- "The QBTR... It's really awful, bro. And I really hope this improves." ([Seibo 2026-09 8:22](https://youtu.be/NqW98hvXUhc?t=502))
- "The one thing holding back your Roblox game are your thumbnails. And two additional things, but your icon and the title of your game as well." ([RoyalDev 2026-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/rxeLNsNUVTQ?t=0))
- "solo developers, anyone that doesn't really want to spend time or money on thumbnails, you can literally do it on your own now." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 3:25](https://youtu.be/xaWGP-slzdM?t=205))
- "The click gates every single other signal, so you should be treating packaging as part of your game." ([Roblox 2026-10 13:07](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=787))
- "No tiny fonts... You want to watch an older player leave, especially on mobile." ([Roblox 2026-10 19:54](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1194))
- "I changed one thing on the thumbnail, and I got 44% more visits... for the exact same ad budget." ([MoseyTube 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/tao925_ypVg?t=0))
- "If you can't tell what the main thing is in 1 second, it's too busy. Make the main thing bigger." ([MoseyTube 2026-10 4:59](https://youtu.be/tao925_ypVg?t=299))
- "There should be at least like the maximum amount I think is 15 for advertising so that you can see exactly which thumbnail is the best." ([kra_DeZ 2026-10 2:13](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=133))
- "Thumbnails that ran through the change without being edited or replaced lost about half their QPTR on the same days." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/home-thumbnail-a-b-test/4947121))
- "Making your Roblox game icons, thumbnails, vector arts just got so much easier with this service." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/EP8W16Y_svA?t=0))
- "It took the exact same reference image. It took the golf ball out and it put Verity in." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 2:47](https://youtu.be/EP8W16Y_svA?t=167))
- "Keep multiple thumbnails active instead of choosing one winner, as this allows personalization to adapt to changing user trends." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails))
- "Avoid placing any essential text or elements at the bottom of the thumbnail, as it may potentially be covered by metadata like the player count." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails))
- "Using a unique event thumbnail that is different from your experience thumbnails will help to increase player engagement with the event." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events))

## [[titles-descriptions]]
- "If you copied what every other game in your genre does, your title and icon probably aren't unique." ([1Coal 2023-08 2:36](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=156))
- "space being a genre in of itself makes it a difficult one" ([GnomeCode 2025-02 8:51](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=531))
- "pay a lot of attention to the title and the description of the items that you make" ([Roblox 2025-09 4:16](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=256))
- "The title gives context to the thumbnail, and the title is what conveys the core fantasy." ([Tizzy 2026-05 7:23](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=443))
- "I'm planning to make it viral, but more on that later in the video." ([LovJak 2026-06 2:07](https://youtu.be/ImcICq5XfL8?t=127))
- "Don't try to give your game a smart name. Be clear instead of being smart." ([rasdev 2026-08 7:52](https://youtu.be/MH9TaOMTm9Q?t=472))
- "Every winning title has to be like a half-second comprehension test for players. You have to be very clear instead of being smart." ([rasdev 2026-09 8:06](https://youtu.be/IyEqyPOjEvU?t=486))
- "every game on Roblox right now is doing the verb a noun trend" ([rasdev 2026-09 1:21](https://youtu.be/Df5ebFGEOXY?t=81))
- "You understand exactly what the game is about before you even play it, you're building a pyramid." ([RoyalDev 2026-09 1:22](https://youtu.be/rxeLNsNUVTQ?t=82))
- "The first ~160 characters of your description is the most critical section as it will be the content that will likely populate search snippets and what new users first see." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/descriptions))
- "Tags and hashtags are explicitly not recommended. They are unused in our ranking algorithms and serve only to eat up your character count limit." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/descriptions))

## [[discovery-algorithm]]
- "Popularity-based recommendations made popular games more popular, making it harder for new developers to have their games discovered." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 5:00](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=300))
- "far and away probably the best way for a game to get discovered is to get picked up by a youtuber" ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 48:16](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=2896))
- "A content explosion means anything you wanted is now a needle in a haystack, so we need recommendation AI to really step up." ([Roblox 2023-07 26:01](https://youtu.be/RNwPOSY8NjI?t=1561))
- "Just make your game hit search for something that doesn't have that many games already there, and it's just free visits." ([SmartyRBX 2023-08 0:22](https://youtu.be/iD_8dYacd-s?t=22))
- "Think about these six criteria and think about whether it's become more engaging or less engaging." ([Roblox 2023-09 24:33](https://youtu.be/V1uFFUUCLpo?t=1473))
- "Engagement by far has the most weights, and we break it down in a couple of ways." ([Roblox 2023-09 5:43](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=343))
- "Build a great experience that users love, tell Roblox about it (via high-quality metadata and media), experiment with finding your audience, and nurture it." ([Roblox DevForum 2024-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111))
- "we design and continue improving our Discovery system according to three principles; the first is a merit-based discovery" ([MonzterDEV 2024-03 5:30](https://youtu.be/em15yK5RjBk?t=330))
- "we optimize Discovery for three main purposes: engagement, monetization and ecosystem health" ([MonzterDEV 2024-03 7:17](https://youtu.be/em15yK5RjBk?t=437))
- "hook home recommendation is the king which means you know the pick up by the Roblox algorithm itself so focusing on the day one and day seven retention" ([Roblox Creators 2024-08 29:06](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1746))
- "we do not use these benchmarks as a direct signal in our discovery algorithm." ([Roblox DevForum 2024-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-new-experience-overview-with-insights-benchmarks-realtime/3108840))
- "These recommendation signals are calculated as averages per user, not total values." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441))
- "Roblox is more fun when you're in experiences with friends... users with higher frequency of play, spend, and interaction with friends have a higher likelihood of long-term retention." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-with-the-improved-recommended-for-you-algorithm-and-analytics-for-creators/3587441))
- "we discovered a lot of popular content that was only published like two days ago." ([Roblox 2025-04 26:21](https://youtu.be/vTc2wciWukE?t=1581))
- "Roblox is looking for three things: high average play time, strong retention, and monetization that doesn't feel spammy or pay to win." ([AlvinBlox 2025-06 7:46](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=466))
- "You can't just ask the algorithm to promote your game. You have to give it some proof." ([AlvinBlox 2025-06 8:28](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=508))
- "Do ads impact organic discovery in any negative way? The answer is no." ([Roblox Creators 2025-06 46:13](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2773))
- "games don't blow up instantly. It usually takes around 2 weeks to hit the algorithm." ([janisjanis01 2025-06 15:10](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=910))
- "Games usually take around a week to hit the algorithm, so don't lose hope on your project." ([janisjanis01 2025-07 18:07](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=1087))
- "I think it is probably skewed towards the bigger games because obviously the games with the most players are going to be played first by new users." ([PineappleDev 2025-07 1:25](https://youtu.be/zaQltaqOV7I?t=85))
- "As long as your game ticks the boxes of what the Roblox algorithm likes, then your game has a pretty good chance like nine times out of 10." ([Erthlord 2025-08 7:03](https://youtu.be/dB6Us5PaWFw?t=423))
- "The players seem to really enjoy this game, but the algorithm doesn't." ([janisjanis01 2025-09 12:06](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=726))
- "How far your game goes, how much organic distribution it gets, depends on the engagement of players who discover it organically." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157))
- "Only if you click and play from the recommended for you section, the signals that drive the home recommendation algorithm are considered for ranking." ([Roblox Creators 2025-10 8:37](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=517))
- "Your game can't be pushed by the Roblox algorithm if it's not in the algorithm to begin with." ([bubDEV 2025-12 0:40](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=40))
- "The game just hit home recommendations. This means that it's finally in the algorithm." ([janisjanis01 2025-12 17:10](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=1030))
- "statistics aren't everything. There's always a little bit of luck involved." ([1_Bruce 2025-12 13:39](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=819))
- "Copyright artist blew up by the algorithm. I didn't even advertise it. It was just because I changed the game icon from a really bad one to a really good one." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-12 1:17:20](https://youtu.be/LyJ04iE6K1M?t=4640))
- "Roblox's goal is to boost certain games so more players discover them, and to put friendly faces to the work." ([Tubefilter 2026-00 ](https://www.tubefilter.com/?p=194084))
- "The algorithm doesn't reward technical elegance. It rewards behavioral efficiency." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606))
- "This does not change how recommendations are ranked, and your game's position is still determined by the same algorithm as before." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571))
- "We don't actually want our creators to be creating sort of deeply addictive and deeply time-consuming experiences." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 10:47](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=647))
- "If your stats are low, your game isn't going to go anywhere." ([janisjanis01 2026-04 4:58](https://youtu.be/xqHXDYbuF2w?t=298))
- "Roblox only wants one thing and that's to keep people on the platform for as long as possible." ([rasdev 2026-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=0))
- "The algo has one job, keep people on Roblox for longer." ([Alex Builds Games 2026-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/wb8XBVFI2i4?t=0))
- "Roblox does not like unwasted potential." ([Alex Builds Games 2026-05 11:40](https://youtu.be/wb8XBVFI2i4?t=700))
- "Only if your game has a good average play time. If your play time's like this, you'll only get a couple hundred players." ([Arow 2026-05 1:23](https://youtu.be/BxK28jVj1MY?t=83))
- "We've found in testing that if short-term engagement is overvalued, the system could disproportionately favor games that win attention with exciting thumbnails but don't deliver long-term value." ([Roblox Newsroom 2026-06 ](https://about.roblox.com/newsroom/2026/06/optimizing-discovery-great-games-reach-millions-players-roblox))
- "I forgot to do something really important, set the game's genre. This alone made the player count spike up to 45." ([janisjanis01 2026-06 11:27](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=687))
- "We look for proof that players are enjoying the game and then recommend it to more players who are also likely to enjoy it." ([Roblox Creators 2026-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/K7jsk3bzmvE?t=0))
- "To best predict whether someone who sees a game organically will enjoy it, we only use signals from players who also discovered the game organically on home." ([Roblox Creators 2026-06 0:42](https://youtu.be/K7jsk3bzmvE?t=42))
- "the Roblox algorithm does not care about my awesome games at all." ([Raul 2026-06 2:42](https://youtu.be/dAL8ohgzN28?t=162))
- "If your game keeps players coming back over time, this algorithm update is designed to reward that more clearly in distribution." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/recommended-for-you-algorithm-improvements-that-better-value-long-term-retention/4684575))
- "Benchmarks and benchmark games do not impact the recommendation algorithm in any way." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/recommended-for-you-algorithm-improvements-that-better-value-long-term-retention/4684575))
- "If your game after 2 weeks does not get home recommendations, I recommend to move on or just leave the game." ([VuukStudios 2026-06 14:06](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=846))
- "PTR is one of the 20 plus signals. It does not solve everything." ([Roblox Creators 2026-06 28:20](https://youtu.be/B3R1ybI3yzs?t=1700))
- "Clickbait is cooked. So, these games that say like mysterious game... you can't do that anymore." ([Tizzy 2026-06 6:01](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=361))
- "They want high-quality games on the platform." ([Tizzy 2026-06 6:01](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=361))
- "you need to know that you need at least 10 minutes of play time in the game to actually get some sort of results." ([VuukStudios 2026-06 13:24](https://youtu.be/nvnSmeP3_xM?t=804))
- "A tiny handful of games take the vast majority of all the payouts, and everyone else splits the scraps." ([Generalist Programmer 2026-07 1:48](https://youtu.be/CFohgybeybM?t=108))
- "Impressions go to CTR, and then that feeds the algorithm with what gets clicked." ([rasdev 2026-07 0:41](https://youtu.be/9_cHgdOo7i0?t=41))
- "If your game didn't hit home recs by itself, even without the all ages, you don't really have any chance of your game hitting the algo." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 11:56](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=716))
- "Now they're watching one thing. Do people come back? Not per week like what it used to be, but over the span of a month." ([waveshine 2026-07 2:21](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=141))
- "we shared with our community our strategic decision to focus our discovery algorithms directly on measured long-term retention." ([Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07 ](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript))
- "optimizing for long-term retention increases the lifetime value of our users: players who stay longer return more often, bring their friends, and engage more deeply." ([Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07 ](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm))
- "Games that are strong in both retention and monetization are best positioned to earn more impressions over time." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-by-building-games-people-want-to-play/4779042))
- "The idea itself can be amazing... But the way you execute it really determines how it sticks to the Roblox algorithm." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 6:51](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=411))
- "The algorithm did its job. It picked the better game." ([rasdev 2026-08 0:40](https://youtu.be/MH9TaOMTm9Q?t=40))
- "The better a game serves its audience, the more our discovery system works for it." ([GamesBeat 2026-09 ](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/))
- "We were optimizing for short-term outcomes for players and finding that they didn't always correlate with long-term outcomes." ([Roblox 2026-09 1:23:48](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=5028))
- "When players come back to your game, the system will keep sending you more." ([Roblox 2026-09 1:26:33](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=5193))
- "Soon games with a strong core loop will be able to break into Select with less than $50 of ad spend." ([Roblox 2026-09 16:12](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=972))
- "Discovery connects our 138 million DAUs to millions of games across our ecosystem." ([Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09 ](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm))
- "Because now that my game's in the algorithm, like I make way more money now." ([Rapstrike GFX 2026-10 1:23](https://youtu.be/7yNr2prDUsE?t=83))
- "algorithm only looked at short-term games, it could favor games that win attention with exciting thumbnails, even if they don't keep players" ([rasdev 2026-10 2:02](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=122))
- "Log the impression only once per session for a specific item. Do not log every time the item appears." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation))
- "Signals are based on users who organically joined from the RFY sort on Home." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq))

## [[updates-live-ops]]
- "every time you shut down the game for an update you risk losing a huge player account" ([Last Level Studios 2019-10 18:45](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1125))
- "Content cadence kind of as this base number through which your game expands and is going to multiply." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 34:33](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2073))
- "If it's at the detriment of the long term health of your game, you should act upon it." ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 31:22](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1882))
- "Nobody wants to have spent 5 hours getting this sword and then suddenly it's nerfed to oblivion." ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 30:41](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1841))
- "when I first started, it was weekly and while it was great for the community... it wasn't so good on my health." ([Roblox Creators 2021-08 52:54](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=3174))
- "Content cadence releases should leverage existing tech and systems only." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:47](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=107))
- "Routine is a good thing for both you and for your players. It tells players that this game is alive and will continue to grow." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 4:38](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=278))
- "Live ops engages the entire Roblox community: existing players, new players following them in, and reactivating older players." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 1:23](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=83))
- "The magic of a live event is that the event runs and modifies the game without ever shutting down." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 8:07](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=487))
- "If you're adding a progression system, I would keep it focused on new content." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 28:10](https://youtu.be/XTZxQvh1qkc?t=1690))
- "I would not automatically assume that smaller more frequent updates are better." ([Roblox Creators 2022-01 31:48](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1908))
- "don't over compensate for one or the other because if you're not releasing enough content updates people aren't going to be as engaged in your game" ([Roblox Creators 2022-06 0:45](https://youtu.be/AkJP8tR0w9Q?t=45))
- "I'd say meaningful updates are much more worthwhile than like frequent updates." ([Roblox Creators 2022-12 29:41](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=1781))
- "You don't need to make content every week every two weeks you'll burn yourself out." ([Roblox Creators 2022-12 27:38](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=1658))
- "power creep is like a little overrated and isn't totally always that bad of a thing" ([Roblox Creators 2022-12 0:06](https://youtu.be/bEEd75mnBIg?t=6))
- "major updates are really important in liveops is that you can add new avenues for these content Cadence releases" ([Roblox Creators 2022-12 2:10](https://youtu.be/bEEd75mnBIg?t=130))
- "If the gameplay is the same one a year from now, players might get bored and drop off." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 11:10](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=670))
- "Small updates and then have like a big update... that'll keep players coming back and especially with the influencers they'll keep creating content on it." ([Roblox 2023-09 19:42](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=1182))
- "Identify areas of improvement with feedback loops and KPIs, then prioritise critical issues that hurt your game's health." ([Roblox 2024-09 12:47](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=767))
- "The real big question is how do we maximize that temporary boost as long as possible." ([Roblox 2024-09 34:55](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=2095))
- "I slowly lost fashion which is a key to success in Roblox development" ([janisjanis01 2025-01 4:44](https://youtu.be/hxjumUFspP0?t=284))
- "We implemented a live service. We started weekly updates. We're known for doing weekly updates in games across Roblox." ([GamesBeat 2025-08 ](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182))
- "We always want to work with our developers first and give them an opportunity to make changes to the design of that game." ([Roblox 2025-08 27:50](https://youtu.be/cuPGe99m8zs?t=1670))
- "I don't think that not updating your game on 6 months on the platform should be rewarded." ([Roblox 2025-09 39:23](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=2363))
- "Strong success: KPIs show a sustained increase, establishing a new, higher baseline after the event or update concludes." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-understand-the-success-of-your-experience-events/4019126))
- "It taught me a ton about doing updates under pressure. Mainly, don't do them." ([DitchyDevelopments 2025-11 2:50](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=170))
- "50 60% of our update ideas just come from the community." ([Roblox 2025-12 1:46](https://youtu.be/oDIpSDOYfY0?t=106))
- "We'll limit breaking changes to once a week, and announce releases a day in advance with change notes." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904))
- "Games that slow their update cadence fall off charts within weeks." ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/top-10-roblox-games-by-players-march-2026-data-breakdown))
- "Static games, no matter how polished, are not going to win chart slots in 2026." ([StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05 ](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/))
- "Release does not mean your game is finished. Your game is only finished when it's dead." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 17:49](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=1069))
- "I would update it even then, guys. I would make the smallest changes, and those changes would actually improve the game and make it even grow." ([kra_DeZ 2026-08 2:42](https://youtu.be/oMI-GhUcfFE?t=162))
- "it was getting only like 20 players active and then like I revived it by just adding small little updates" ([kra_DeZ 2026-08 4:07](https://youtu.be/g6z18oHjbYE?t=247))
- "Updates don’t. You publish, and people find out whenever they next open the app - even if they follow the game." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4850631))
- "We do just have a very strong core loop and the foundations of the game are good enough to sustain the game without us constantly just shoveling in whatever we can." ([Roblox Creators 2026-09 36:43](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2203))
- "Every day I'm talking. You got to add updates. You got to see what people are liking and what they're not liking." ([kra_DeZ 2026-10 10:12](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=612))
- "Updates like "bug fixes" are vague and don't inform the reader of what was actually fixed." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events))
- "it can be much harder to regain a user's interest than it can be to maintain active ones." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization))
- "Spending fewer than three weeks' effort on content cadence is recommended in order to maintain a rapid update schedule and leave room for other LiveOps releases." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/content-updates))
- "Adding new systems to a game to support new deliverables turns the content into an expansion, which would be unsustainable for the creation of a content cadence." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/content-updates))

## [[social-features-and-virality]]
- "one of the key things I want to convey is that any game can be social" ([Roblox 2018-07 9:02](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=542))
- "definitely encourage you guys to reserve the slots so that people can join their friends or just use the default" ([Roblox 2018-07 11:07](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=667))
- "David said if I wanted to walk over and talk to Jay party I can literally walk over and talk to Jay party." ([TheDevKing 2020-07 0:43](https://youtu.be/lxOtRYuNqIw?t=43))
- "When communicating with your friends our older players will have much more freedom than they have today." ([TheDevKing 2020-07 0:43](https://youtu.be/lxOtRYuNqIw?t=43))
- "player density where you get as many players together in a small space and then you get lots of high frequency social interactions" ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 8:15](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=495))
- "instead focus on platforms like Tick Tock and Twitter and create fun and engaging content that will get people excited about playing your game" ([SmartyRBX 2023-01 2:01](https://youtu.be/DOMuVNlHv8Y?t=121))
- "Eventually as a dev, you kind of have to let that go and just accept, hey, this is viral marketing, I guess." ([Roblox Creators 2023-04 2:49](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=169))
- "consistently posting stuff that doesn't even take five minutes to make will literally just bring a consistent flow of players" ([ItsZyonic 2023-06 0:40](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=40))
- "TikTok has over 1 billion users, so it's where most of the attention is on the internet, but very few people are using it that effectively." ([SmartyRBX 2023-08 1:55](https://youtu.be/iD_8dYacd-s?t=115))
- "You have to have a very good game for this, and you have to offer them a ton of value." ([SmartyRBX 2023-08 3:30](https://youtu.be/iD_8dYacd-s?t=210))
- "Influencers can help experiences take off before our recommendation engine actually picks it up." ([Roblox 2023-09 1:35](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=95))
- "For a lot of creators it's quite isolating... so much of the enjoyment is our engagement with them." ([Roblox 2023-09 23:07](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=1387))
- "We've seen about, I think, a third of the top 1,000 developers adopt this over the last year. It's just the graph is going up into the right." ([Roblox 2023-09 14:17](https://youtu.be/V1uFFUUCLpo?t=857))
- "The video went from zero views to 150,000 in just 1 hour." ([1Coal 2024-03 6:16](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=376))
- "When you find others you play more you discover more you spend more." ([Roblox 2024-09 2:37](https://youtu.be/LMUQpm0HpEQ?t=157))
- "We know that voice engagement leads to more playtime and more spending in experiences." ([Roblox 2024-09 9:50](https://youtu.be/LMUQpm0HpEQ?t=590))
- "What triggers viral moments is something unexpected or something new." ([Roblox 2024-09 23:57](https://youtu.be/LMUQpm0HpEQ?t=1437))
- "Even if people from other regions come in, the more you get with other French speakers, the more they talk about it on their social channels." ([Roblox 2024-09 33:03](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1983))
- "if I was particularly interested in earning as much as possible from this program then my main focus would be thinking about what content I could create which would go viral on social media platforms" ([MonzterDEV 2024-09 2:38](https://youtu.be/wbDsGk-Te14?t=158))
- "this is probably the most important tip in the entire video you want to add a button in your game that invites friends" ([waveshine 2024-10 2:34](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=154))
- "Understand Roblox is a social platform. That's why 99% of the time, singleplayer games fail on Roblox." ([SmartyRBX 2025-04 3:41](https://youtu.be/N2FND74tgGE?t=221))
- "We were measuring both safety and freedom at the same time." ([Roblox 2025-07 15:19](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=919))
- "Players who co-play stay longer, return more often, and drive higher DAU. They often end up spending almost twice as much in your experiences." ([Roblox 2025-09 9:36](https://youtu.be/lUc3Xj8Ojd8?t=576))
- "On supported devices, players can record short clips up to 30 seconds long right from your experience, and it's on by default." ([Roblox 2025-09 16:04](https://youtu.be/lUc3Xj8Ojd8?t=964))
- "I decided to make like a long form of drawing people that I did on TikTok and just make that into a YouTube video and then it really that video just like instant well almost instantly took off" ([Creators on the Blox 2025-11 9:32](https://youtu.be/f9BmInLHotA?t=572))
- "The biggest barrier between collabs happening is just asking. You can get away with a lot of stuff if you just ask nicely." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-11 42:54](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=2574))

- "I think this method works. You just need way more views and more followers." ([janisjanis01 2026-01 12:21](https://youtu.be/IWzAHgHPhhU?t=741))
- "We're expanding Trusted Friends so when friends and family from different age groups age check and become Trusted Friends, they can chat together on Roblox." - "An empty city full of friends will beat a packed dungeon full of strangers, every single time." ([StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05 ](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/))

- "The most powerful discovery engine on Roblox are the people who play your games." ([Roblox 2026-09 1:31:23](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=5483))
- "So, I recommend being in a public server. Definitely make your life easier." ([revolver agz 2026-09 34:36](https://youtu.be/j3L0K1yZjms?t=2076))

## [[analytics-metrics]]
- "N.B Because of Roblox, acquisition is not accurate so uhhh, don't use that." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083))

- "Minutes spent or engagement is your best proxy for love. Time doesn't lie." ([Roblox 2018-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=730))

- "Do not only make choices based on data!" ([Roblox DevForum 2020-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057))

- "Avoid items that create a new source of resources and might unbalance things, or at the very least, build in limits." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 25:28](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1528))
- "certain competitions, if a user plays them, they're more likely to come back the next day." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 31:55](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1915))
- "a sprint button... actually like massively reduced day one retention so maybe people were running past each other" ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 28:17](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=1697))

- "Building an experience without analytics is like driving a car to an unknown place without navigation." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-kpis-and-lower-enrollment-announcing-the-analytics-dashboard-measure-track-make-data-driven-decisions/1688794))

- "For example, for day 1 retention, the 50th to 90th percentile range for similar experiences might be 12.4% - 24.1%." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-similar-experience-benchmarks-broader-access/2210285))
- "You can't do that if you don't know what's going on in your game. You don't have the insight to see where do I need to iterate." ([Roblox Creators 2023-06 2:09](https://youtu.be/pWF06iX9_Gg?t=129))

- "Your new benchmark scorecard helps you focus on the right metrics to improve compared to similar experiences." ([Roblox DevForum 2024-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-new-experience-overview-with-insights-benchmarks-realtime/3108840))
