- "Set XP as one currency and Warrior, Mage, Paladin as Custom Field 1 instead of separate currencies." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types))
- "You should use custom fields whenever possible instead of event names, since there is a much tighter cardinality limit on event names than custom fields." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events))
- "Events can only be sent from the server and in published games. Events can't be sent from the client or Studio." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/custom-events))
- "Let experiments run for their full durations. The novelty effect can heavily skew early results, sometimes causing them to swing in and out of statistical significance." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments))
- "If a change isn't statistically significant, ignore it." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments))
- "The model expects a realistic ratio. If you serve 1 impression but log 10 plays from external traffic, you confuse the training data." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/recommendation))
- "Note that if a metric is at a six month high but below benchmark comparisons, you should still continue to improve it." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights))
- "Analytics and player feedback reports use AI and can make mistakes. Make sure to check responses and weigh player feedback against your judgment." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights))
- "The two metrics that you need to focus on are opt-in rate and daily frequency." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/tycoon))
- "Developers should strive for an at least 80% like ratio after the close of a beta program." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences))

## [[playtesting-and-feedback]]
- "It's okay if you don't do things the right time just keep playtesting and iterate and then also test on real devices." ([Roblox 2018-07 17:51](https://youtu.be/B3oqg1il2tI?t=1071))
- "Players will help you improve and grow. Be dauntless, do not fear criticism, do not be afraid of having a bad game." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-game/287493))
- "nobody played it because i'd done a bad job of the actual customization system but as i fixed things more and more people started playing" ([Roblox 2020-11 5:11](https://youtu.be/GqleMaqFhPE?t=311))
- "Your game will always be easier for you than for most of your players." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 15:30](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=930))
- "Using just one of these only one leads to blind spots, so it's necessary to combine multiple research methods to get good data to inform design." ([GDC Festival of Gaming 2022-02 4:13](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=253))
- "Analytics is fantastic at telling us what players did but it's often very poor at telling us why they did that." ([GDC Festival of Gaming 2022-02 49:25](https://youtu.be/WGRpglQ87XE?t=2965))
- "Scrapped entirely; we found out it just did not play well, the stairs were just not fun to go up." ([Roblox Creators 2022-03 36:50](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=2210))
- "you put 10000 15000 20000 people on across different devices across the globe" ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 29:28](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=1768))
- "on twitter or instagram or any sort of social network i'll get all of that feedback and some more which can be really useful in a game design perspective" ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 0:47](https://youtu.be/e9II73see-8?t=47))
- "ignoring your players will only lead a frustration and a decline in play years ass so you gotta fix issues fast" ([SmartyRBX 2023-02 1:19](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=79))
- "QA testing shouldn't be the last step in your development process, when everything is completed and ready for release." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838))
- "Don't scare testers by saying 'no duplicates' hundreds of times during your test." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838))
- "Take a step back. If you're developing for console, put yourselves in the shoes of someone using it ten feet away." ([Roblox Creators 2023-05 13:27](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=807))
- "The people giving that kind of feedback on a platform like YouTube are people who are more familiar with the game." ([Roblox 2023-09 16:16](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=976))
- "look at them flying let's see what they think of it... my game is just broke" ([ItsZyonic 2023-12 15:05](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=905))
- "My biggest lesson learned is to listen to the players and not just qualitatively; knowing who your players are is so important." ([GameMakers 2024-03 13:48](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=828))
- "the actual Target users will play differently and will have different feedback" ([Roblox Creators 2024-08 27:43](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1663))
- "If this game was like more polished, it would actually be a pretty fun game." ([TheDankDeveloper 2024-10 6:08](https://youtu.be/F2vhC3AuZyk?t=368))
- "No highlights, no hints, no nothing, just a pure game." ([ByteBlox 2025-01 30:21](https://youtu.be/K-KvVwt_Y4I?t=1821))
- "for every player that comes to you, there are hundreds that have just refused to talk to you" ([Roblox 2025-09 36:10](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=2170))
- "I want to be genuinely good instead of just oh I do stuff other people don't do. It's good as a video, not just because no one else is doing this." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-10 35:48](https://youtu.be/WODeLtB2YH4?t=2148))
- "Create situations where you can watch players when they don't know they are being observed." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940))
- "I made this in a I made it in one and a half hours." ([TheDankDeveloper 2025-12 11:31](https://youtu.be/D6Ej1CgiWOc?t=691))
- "by yourself people lose interest... but when you're with a team... they would become addicted." ([Daiorg 2025-12 39:58](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2398))
- "your work that you've done is never good enough." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-06 1:04:58](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=3898))
- "It's very common to become developer blind, which is this concept that you played and developed your own game for so long, you can no longer tell what a new player would experience." ([Yann 2026-06 6:03](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=363))
- "If you're not at the top of the leaderboard in terms of play time for your own game, you're not testing enough." ([Tizzy 2026-07 4:46](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=286))
- "These kids play the game more than me or Gage have ever played the game." ([Tizzy 2026-08 8:44](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=524))
- "Majority of the people thought the concept was just flat-out stupid." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 8:51](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=531))
- "I took this to heart and I worked on fixing these points, and I released a big update which fixed a lot of the questions and feedback that the wider community was asking for." ([studio rook. 2026-08 2:37](https://youtu.be/EuaV0qc6UXQ?t=157))
- "Your test build does something unexpected, and you make a note to fix it in the morning. Try playing it first." ([Roblox 2026-09 19:26](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=1166))
- "If you get your fans to play it, they're probably gonna lie to you about how much they like it. Or be extremely critical." ([Roblox Creators 2026-09 28:28](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=1708))
- "True directors break vague complaints into concrete tasks." ([mats-dept2 2026-10 2:11](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=131))
- "invite up to 10 friends or Group members as early testers to preview their game on Roblox before making your game public." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-simpler-path-to-publish-plus-invite-early-testers/4930524))
- "Do closed testing with people who will give you real feedback, not friends who will say you built the best game ever." ([Roblox 2026-10 18:15](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1095))
- "It's a good thing I did that because I found a few errors, bro. So now I have to fix those bugs." ([tef 2026-10 13:25](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=805))
- "Each number that a game designer uses for something in their game is an educated guess based on what might be fun." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project))
- "Always test to see if it feels fun, and ask friends to play-test it as well." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project))
- "Non-performant games may struggle to grow and maintain a user base." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies))
- "If your studio is unclear on which option is best, Roblox Education recommends closed access testing. This method tends to be the safest." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences))

## [[scripting-and-performance]]
- "Wouldn't sending server updates every render step end up using a decent amount of traffic on the server and on the client?" ([Roblox DevForum 2018-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434))
- "It's not good practice to put the player's name in the DataStore; it's best to use their user ID." ([AlvinBlox 2018-08 5:12](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=312))
- "When a function references a variable from an outside scope it holds a strong reference to that value!" ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954))
- "Use the dot product instead of magnitude to avoid unnecessary square root calculations, which are expensive." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943))
- "i'm not a big fan of loops running all the time in the background it generally implies some sort of bad programming practice" ([Last Level Studios 2020-08 30:33](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1833))
- "Sine is closer to the Linear EasingStyle than any other EasingStyle." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-easing-styles/937281))
- "Quint is the fastest, Quart is the second fastest, and Quad is the slowest." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-easing-styles/937281))
- "I wouldn't just be blindly following a tutorial; I was learning the guts and what made these programs tick." ([AlvinBlox 2021-01 0:31](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=31))
- "The loop style is extremely inefficient and will cause major lag throughout your game." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/open-source-lock-on-combat-in-3d/1458506))
- "It's literally just easier to make your own GUI system than it is to play with the Roblox default." ([Last Level Studios 2021-12 17:02](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=1022))
- "it's very important that it says prompt game pass purchase because... i just used prop product purchase and it never worked" ([B Ricey 2022-04 9:08](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=548))
- "Only replicate VFX not hitboxes or itll be vulnerable to exploiters" ([Roblox DevForum 2022-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487))
- "Server sided VFX not only increases server load but also increases ping tremendously." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487))
- "Abstract complexity out as much as possible and only think very hard about what is being exposed to the community." ([Roblox 2022-06 40:39](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=2439))
- "rather than resuming event handlers immediately we cue them up and resume them all at once this improves performance" ([Crusherfire 2024-01 2:00](https://youtu.be/xz_G2n1Q8-U?t=120))
- "I don't think I'm trusting CH GTP again" ([janisjanis01 2024-03 4:08](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=248))
- "Literally the only way to prevent it is just to not script it in in the first place... memory leaks only occur if you mess something up while coding." ([ByteBlox 2024-05 4:35](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=275))
- "once you start getting into the thousands ... you're going to want to have a more efficient way of figuring out what trees or whatever objects are closest to the player" ([Crusherfire 2024-05 0:42](https://youtu.be/OksI7HpEd6w?t=42))
- "it's always good to handle this stuff on the server because it's authoritative... can't be touched by exploiters" ([AlvinBlox 2024-06 2:32](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=152))
- "ended up being the pet follow script and now the experience is lag free" ([janisjanis01 2024-06 14:35](https://youtu.be/e725ByFD8EI?t=875))
- "Android's always at the center of our focus when pushing an update." ([Roblox 2024-09 27:19](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1639))
- "most crashes in Roblox are really out of memory errors" ([Roblox 2024-09 26:26](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1586))
- "Don't just quadruple all of your lights... it's not good for optimization and it will crash your game." ([Roblox 2024-09 21:28](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1288))
- "the text kind of appears letter by letter... and then you're also able to click and you know quickly finish it" ([ByteBlox 2025-01 6:40](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=400))
- "Thankfully, if you're lazy like me, Roblox has the .Unit method for you to just get the unit-vector without having to normalize it manually." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/math-for-roblox-developers-1-vectors/3659072))
- "Calling it once at the beginning of the session doesn't mean that they can watch five or 10 ads." ([Roblox Creators 2025-08 25:21](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1521))
- "Bytecode is the final boss of debugging → beat it once, and nothing in Roblox will ever feel mysterious again." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941))
- "If you paste private code into web tools, don't include API keys or secure code." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941))
- "The recommendation is to use preferred input over last input unless you really want to know for some reason that there was a mouse click or a specific keyboard stroke." ([Roblox 2025-09 28:08](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1688))
- "Understanding how different parts of a script work together, not just learning each concept individually." ([ArTheDev 2026-03 4:36](https://youtu.be/R3q6hZs8FDE?t=276))
- "Collections automatically manage connections so their callbacks are leak-free." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143))
- "Using a rootContext with a small subtree of your place is the best way to maximize Collection query performance." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143))
- "We found that this alone reduced cheating prevalence by 97% on Android." ([Roblox 2026-09 53:33](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3213))
- "Scripting is the main thing of any game. If the scripting is ass, the game is ass." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 10:27](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=627))
- "Memory is fatal... having performance that is a little slower is not as much. I would always pick memory first." ([Roblox 2026-10 14:55](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=895))
- "Mobile VR is sensitive to a high number of draw calls." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines))
- "We recommend that you do not cache the results of your GetUsersPriceLevelsAsync API calls after a user session. User price levels can change." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing))

## [[building-art-and-assets]]
- "Most agree that they were crap at building until they used plugins, get to know the keyboard shortcuts." ([Roblox DevForum 2016-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919))
- "Don't treat CSG as this magical performance fixing solution. Unions every pair of parts in my world surely it must become more efficient, no." ([Roblox 2018-07 31:22](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1882))
- "Lighting... it's considered last minute of polish... it should never be considered polish in your very core can help route everything together." ([Roblox 2018-07 23:21](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1401))
- "Cadence is all about the art. It should be simple, relatively easy to produce art, mostly things that you already have in the game." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 9:56](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=596))
- "When the temperature rises more evaporation will occur creating more clouds." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dynamic-weather-library-beta-10/1547614))
- "This project is in Beta, and will most likely not be released even until 2022. It will be updating as Roblox's clouds update as well." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dynamic-weather-library-beta-10/1547614))
- "Gray boxing is just placing a gray block and shaping it to match what you're going for; you have not much to lose." ([Roblox Creators 2022-03 30:51](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1851))
- "I was really surprised how few developers when I spoke to them knew how to use them and they are just amazing." ([Roblox Creators 2023-03 0:30](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=30))
- "It is understandable is far more important than it being stylized or pretty." ([Roblox Creators 2023-03 6:40](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=400))
- "If it fits in that, it fits on mobile. Anything else goes in the physical world." ([Roblox Creators 2023-03 14:13](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=853))
- "lighting is very underused in a lot of games and it can make such a big difference" ([Roblox Creators 2023-03 1:27](https://youtu.be/CmXyiJw5N_Y?t=87))
- "white text on yellow. That's a good example that don't do that. Please don't do that." ([Roblox Creators 2023-04 1:10](https://youtu.be/uumqDp020jk?t=70))
- "no matter what your color palette is, unless it's literally just yellow and white, in which case we should have a talk." ([Roblox Creators 2023-04 1:31](https://youtu.be/syQcaeRqqc4?t=91))
- "If you set everything to a grayscale... if you can't tell the difference from the monochrome, a colorblind person can't." ([Roblox Creators 2023-05 29:34](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=1774))
- "Colorblind filters generally make things look muddy. Contrast sliders are probably your best bet, even if it makes things look a bit dull." ([Roblox Creators 2023-05 14:48](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=888))
- "Breaking your paragraphs up into bite-sized pieces with new space between things is the best practice for readability." ([Roblox Creators 2023-05 18:10](https://youtu.be/5Ex0tW-n9_w?t=1090))
- "you make money each time rather than a commission which is one time for one person" ([SmartyRBX 2023-06 3:45](https://youtu.be/74VNexodi10?t=225))
- "If your game is looking like Minecraft 2009 the players just want to enjoy playing it and most likely will leave after a few minutes." ([janisjanis01 2023-08 0:25](https://youtu.be/fRBNHaMtvFs?t=25))
- "Every imperfection in our creations is highlighted when we don't do things the right way." ([Roblox 2023-09 5:16](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=316))
- "ensure that your ad can actually be viewed" ([Roblox 2023-09 28:35](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=1715))
- "This is actually the most fun I've had building on Roblox and I think it's because the studs just make it similar to Lego." ([Mulch 2024-07 2:02](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=122))
- "Translate is sort of table stakes; then there's all this other stuff on top to make it feel native to the user." ([Roblox 2024-09 23:14](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1394))
- "it goes to show that you can make money and make successful Roblox games without much scripting experience or building experience" ([CallMeDogey 2025-01 2:34](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=154))
- "it actually makes building environments so easy all you have to do is select which parts you want to be duplicating" ([Quez 2025-02 2:45](https://youtu.be/hCW2OhUBPf4?t=165))
- "when you see a Lobby like this you know the game is good and if it if it's not then something very weird is going on" ([GnomeCode 2025-02 12:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=770))
- "I asked the AI to generate me some ideas, but they were garbage. So, I used my own brain and created multiple unique designs." ([janisjanis01 2025-03 6:38](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=398))
- "check out some like before you're even worried about modeling anything, check out some tutorials on just how to use the interface because there's a lot of hotkeys in Blender." ([Roblox Creators 2025-07 8:14](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=494))
- "So this is how you squeeze a lot out of a little." ([Roblox Creators 2025-07 31:37](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=1897))
- "The bigger the number, it'll be harder to read for a lot of people." ([Stewiepfing 2025-08 5:28](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=328))
- "Shout out cuz I learned that from Deepop's videos. I haven't seen anyone else do it so I think made it." ([#7Phantom 2025-09 4:17](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=257))
- "clone stamping actually makes the quality worse if you didn't know. So you got to add some noise to make it look good again." ([#7Phantom 2025-09 9:19](https://youtu.be/9Ins2jyZlTc?t=559))
- "Ideally heads would be dynamic and 3D going forward because this has the best support for makeup and facial animation." ([Roblox 2025-09 26:03](https://youtu.be/Tese1QLD3nU?t=1563))
- "As you build confidence in the system once it is released on all platforms, you should feel liberated to use more unique textures in your experience." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855))
- "Texture streaming will work better with individual, unique textures, if they are not batchable." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855))
- "having the very simple art style means things are just organically more clear." ([Daiorg 2025-12 33:52](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2032))
- "We call them 4D assets... geometry and the behavior should be generated together, because a door should open and a helicopter should fly." ([Roblox 2026-02 4:11](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=251))
- "If you're going to have low budget aesthetics, you got to at least make up for it with gameplay." ([Cumin 2026-06 5:20](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=320))
- "Stylized visuals, simple gameplay loops, memes, and consistency always will beat high-end graphic games." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 3:26](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=206))
- "If you enable this system your own LODs will have LODs. You don't want that. So disable your user side LODs." ([Roblox 2026-10 24:15](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1455))
- "Most of the things don't actually work in real game settings. And for it to work in game settings, it has to be segmented correctly." ([Roblox 2026-10 16:41](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1001))
- "don't rely too heavily on AI's builds... AI isn't that great at making builds, as well as UI. Make sure you always give it a reference." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 4:47](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=287))

## [[ai-and-tools]]
- "Please use the DevForum to either show your support for feature requests related to accessibility or make those requests directly." ([Roblox Creators 2023-05 1:00:04](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=3604))
- "this is NOT a proper anti-cheat for your games!" ([Roblox DevForum 2024-01 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460))
- "you'd be better off not using ML in this example, but there are plenty of situations where you definitely could & should" ([Roblox DevForum 2024-01 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460))
- "We want to be basically your dishwasher, so we want to let you focus on creativity." ([Roblox 2024-09 4:51](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=291))
- "Start early, fail early, play around." ([Roblox 2024-09 27:17](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1637))
- "The AI put it in the wrong order; it listens for future coins, so the coins already added never worked." ([ByteBlox 2025-01 7:52](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=472))
- "We will never implement an AI algorithm unless it is more accurate than humans." ([Roblox 2025-07 2:26](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=146))
- "AI is a tool, not a teacher. Vibe Coder is a very handy tool for helping make Roblox games, not for creating them." ([Duckable 2025-08 12:11](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=731))
- "We view AI as nothing but an extension of [the] building blocks. We hope that it'll be a power up for you all." ([Roblox 2025-09 4:24](https://youtu.be/3h25HUtBHFk?t=264))
- "Use AI as a tool to help guide you... make a full roadmap... Obviously don't use AI to make full entire scripts for you." ([VuukStudios 2025-11 12:25](https://youtu.be/U4nduRcszJ4?t=745))
- "Lemonade is a really powerful tool. Most of the game was made by Lemonade." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-01 10:52](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=652))
- "I didn't have to paste any scripts or anything. I think it did everything for me." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-01 4:38](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=278))
- "none of them really match my game, so I don't think I'll be taking any of them." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-04 4:44](https://youtu.be/i5sldtdF8m0?t=284))
- "now that my game is full of UI, it's kind of hard to change." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-04 4:44](https://youtu.be/i5sldtdF8m0?t=284))
- "The magical of the planning mode is to understand the creator intent and give creator control." ([Roblox 2026-04 12:26](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=746))
- "So, all this has to do is start the 1-hour timer and press enter." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/Ufr0SVq-o98?t=0))
- "the thing with Gemini is I actually have to import everything myself." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-04 7:27](https://youtu.be/Ufr0SVq-o98?t=447))
- "I'm saving probably 80% of the time I'd normally spend scripting. Things that would probably take me a whole day are getting done in like 20 minutes." ([MoseyTube 2026-04 2:04](https://youtu.be/sFr7htxsnR4?t=124))
- "AI didn't make me dumber. It just made me forget how it felt to struggle." ([Duckable 2026-08 21:21](https://youtu.be/Cc7HT3uNjeQ?t=1281))
- "We're not using AI to make art assets... we use it to help fill the gaps with our programmers for speed." ([Business of Creators 2026-08 21:06](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=1266))
- "Most of this game is made using like Claude... Most of it was literally just AI." ([kra_DeZ 2026-08 10:11](https://youtu.be/oMI-GhUcfFE?t=611))
- "If you're getting advanced code from these systems, there's zero debugging capability that you have." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 0:40](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=40))
- "it can replace a ton of packages in your workflow, honestly. And this is what I've been using for a while now, and I love it." ([Possible Panda 2026-09 9:12](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=552))
- "if you're worried about losing your job as a programmer to AI, the best thing you can do is learn how to use AI" ([SimTek Game Development 2026-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/-0H6JRtrA2Y?t=0))
- "The time it takes to go from an idea to a playable game is compressing dramatically, and so is the time it takes to adapt a game for new devices." ([Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09 ](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm))
- "Stop making AI slop... this means stop producing and misusing the AI." ([VuukStudios 2026-10 10:49](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=649))
- "What works really well is when you're working with MCP is to give it several images and to tell it, hey, this is the style that I want." ([Roblox 2026-10 42:39](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=2559))
- "it actually doesn't cheat. It doesn't see for instance you're in the same server as all the players." ([Roblox 2026-10 19:19](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1159))
- "It doesn't mean you can just sit back and have everything ready... it's important that you, as a creator, put your own mind into it." ([MonarchDev 2026-10 11:32](https://youtu.be/QwdZRB6xRKk?t=692))
- "Most AI models, even connected to MCP, can't do that just yet. And when they do, it's not the quality you expect." ([MonarchDev 2026-10 10:50](https://youtu.be/QwdZRB6xRKk?t=650))
- "We always prefer to build on top of Roblox's analytic stack instead of rebuilding the basics ourselves." ([Roblox 2026-10 15:27](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=927))
- "Players told me that they were not artists and they wanted help in creating their vision, but they were also clear about not wanting AI use." ([Roblox 2026-10 17:41](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=1061))
- "As you get better at it, each time ask it to explain how it did a particular thing." ([MonarchDev 2026-10 8:22](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=502))
- "I'm going to be doing everything step-by-step like I showed you guys in my last video." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/Mftq7v8nAcs?t=0))
- "I think the AI is doing a pretty good job." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 4:50](https://youtu.be/Mftq7v8nAcs?t=290))
- "I'm never letting AI configure this again. It's a nightmare." ([janisjanis01 2026-10 8:57](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=537))
- "You can't simply say, "Oh, yeah, make this better." What does it even mean?" ([MonarchDev 2026-10 2:26](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=146))
- "As the mods or decompilings get better and better, players are going to stop being able to notice when it is because AI used to equal slop." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 33:27](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=2007))

## [[team-and-budget]]
- "Use Roblox DataStores freely; the platform doesn't impose a limit, which lets apps like Lua Learning exist." ([Last Level Studios 2020-02 31:40](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=1900))
- "Royalty does not scale when you need to hire team members." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475))
- "Make sure you are only hiring team players, not someone who will try to do all the work alone!" ([Roblox DevForum 2020-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475))
- "Creating that culture from the top in the company... the people working for you look at the habits of who hired them." ([Roblox Creators 2022-01 15:16](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=916))
- "When handling other developers, you will never get what you expect, you get what you inspect." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578))
- "Having like the majority of your team be people you can trust unconditionally and talk to, like just brainstorm at midnight and 1:00 in the morning" ([Roblox 2022-09 2:48](https://youtu.be/jULYHFTmq4s?t=168))
- "For Brands it's not like hey I have this project manager who's going to give you guys like five hours a week; that's just not going to fly." ([Roblox 2023-09 27:26](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1646))
- "don't do it by yourself because it's long journey again and it's pretty lonely" ([Roblox Creators 2024-08 35:14](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=2114))
- "You just need to get a few thousand users through the funnel and do a soft launch." ([GameMakers 2024-10 37:46](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2266))
- "accepting percentage of a game that's never been out and the owner has zero track record... you expect not to get scammed" ([VuukStudios 2024-12 9:23](https://youtu.be/xBPJnMgLoUE?t=563))
- "I would recommend Rodev's hidden devs or my developer server where you can get commissions really quickly." ([Arow 2025-05 0:30](https://youtu.be/JeBDqH7INjY?t=30))
- "Commissions are low leverage, know that while you're doing them and have the plan, have the long-term career strategy to be able to move on from that." ([SmartyRBX 2025-06 6:41](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=401))
- "If you can't punish your devs, you will never hold them accountable. You will never keep your team in check." ([SmartyRBX 2025-09 5:57](https://youtu.be/ysqeKxPqBXA?t=357))
- "Retention is loyalty. So, they have to trust you." ([Roblox 2025-09 28:16](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=1696))
- "You should speak with every single creator that you can meet, that you can reach that has sold their game or part of their game to that group to see what their experience was." ([Roblox 2025-09 16:43](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=1003))
- "Most people are just in it for the money, and so if you have money it's easy to get them to work." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-11 12:27](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=747))
- "The most common skills I recommend to people, especially beginners, is either scripting or building." ([VuukStudios 2026-02 0:38](https://youtu.be/JQqhUWZUGDQ?t=38))
- "It has a little bit of a less stricter criteria in terms of accepting people." ([VuukStudios 2026-02 5:02](https://youtu.be/JQqhUWZUGDQ?t=302))
- "I didn't want to just hire people without being able to pay them consistently." ([DitchyDevelopments 2026-03 2:05](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=125))
- "I've never coached another 3D modeler... cuz it means that the art style is currently cohesive." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-03 14:29](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=869))
- "You don't need a huge team. The entire player base and game engine, it's all already there." ([Roblox 2026-04 1:27](https://youtu.be/IuJWzpvpIa8?t=87))
- "Ideas are everywhere, that's not the bottleneck. Market research is not the bottleneck. It's finding good talent that can actually execute on time." ([Tizzy 2026-05 1:21](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=81))
- "New today means not payable today." ([Generalist Programmer 2026-07 4:18](https://youtu.be/XzxoIlpBnJA?t=258))
- "The bottleneck I've found in this business of Roblox is talent, because ideas are everywhere." ([Tizzy 2026-07 3:52](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=232))
- "Instead of paying someone up front, we'd offer him a percentage of the game." ([Lnor Roblox 2026-07 1:11](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=71))
- "You just need to identify the one thing that you're weak at and pay someone specifically for that piece instead of trying to force yourself to be good at everything." ([MoseyTube 2026-08 1:23](https://youtu.be/5zz5ao0zV_Y?t=83))
- "Don't let one bad experience stop you from working with people." ([Raul 2026-09 4:47](https://youtu.be/GLmlPsYLsbM?t=287))
- "It shouldn't even matter if someone is verified or not." ([Raul 2026-09 4:47](https://youtu.be/GLmlPsYLsbM?t=287))
- "Every single week, I get new calls from triple-A studios wanting to talk to me about the opportunity of Roblox." ([GamesBeat 2026-09 ](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-makes-a-play-for-triple-a-developers-at-rdc-2026/))
- "People are relying on your word. If you are saying, I'm going to pay you X amount of dollars on X amount of day, you better do it." ([Tizzy 2026-09 7:43](https://youtu.be/E_tVm3bc5Xo?t=463))
- "The most important thing he's learned since is that you cannot do this alone." ([Roblox 2026-09 1:07:54](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=4074))
- "Don't stop referrals. Run a consistent process. Then you can jam referrals right through the process." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 17:03](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=1023))
- "Culture add, not culture fit. What is missing from your culture this person can bring versus cloning like everybody else." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 20:36](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=1236))
- "If your team sucks, it's your fault. Take ownership." ([Tizzy 2026-10 19:54](https://youtu.be/_JyiKfNNqls?t=1194))

## [[development-process-and-speed]]
- "No game gets popular straight away, mine took over a year. Don't rush your builds, put time and effort into them." ([Roblox DevForum 2016-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919))
- "We are jogging, not sprinting!" ([Roblox DevForum 2017-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838))
- "Your first pass localization will pretty much be a weekend project, but getting it right can take a lot more effort." ([Roblox 2018-07 2:21](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=141))
- "Polishing animations by playing them over and over is annoying, playtesting over and over to make small tweaks is maddening." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782))
- "Lots of tedious task that would take 30 minutes turned into seconds." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057))
- "You should never be spending 1 to 3 months of resources on your team for something that you're going to ship and then just leave to die in your game." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 5:16](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=316))
- "If you're looking to release the perfect feature, you're risking never shipping that feature because there's always something that you can do better on the feature." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 10:40](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=640))
- "Ultimately your goal should be to release when ready, not just because players are demanding it." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 26:04](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1564))
- "If it's game breaking I must replace it; if it's not game breaking I keep it in as long as humanly possible." ([Last Level Studios 2021-08 6:33](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=393))
- "For me as an economy designer, the only thing that matters is the amount of effort, a.k.a. time that goes into a player completing a feature." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 43:01](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2581))
- "Content cadence should not introduce new features, currencies or one off mechanics. Save those big undertakings for your game expansions." ([Roblox Creators 2021-10 0:29](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=29))
- "If people aren't playing your game from the get-go it's maybe time to move on." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 28:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1724))
- "Just do it... don't be so worried about making everything perfect." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 41:52](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=2512))
- "Wireframes are probably your best friend in actually designing UI." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 3:31](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=211))
- "The crunch game dev cycle is just not sustainable in live ops." ([Roblox Creators 2022-01 16:40](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1000))
- "prototypes are critical for detecting design flaws and oversights early in development" ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 0:13](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=13))
- "these are heavily under utilized by developers out there" ([Roblox Creators 2022-02 2:14](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=134))
- "don't get caught up in the just one more of this just one more of that before i release this game" ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:04:45](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=3885))
- "Prototype is the first milestone you want to hit and MVP is the last one you want to hit before you eventually ship your game." ([Roblox Creators 2022-06 35:13](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2113))
- "For this reason, do not aim for perfection in a game, aim for completion." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578))
- "if you don't finish your game it's never going to be out and the whole goal should be to finish your game" ([SmartyRBX 2022-09 4:11](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=251))
- "Making that event was the hardest thing that we've ever done out of all of the things that we've been doing since we started creating." ([Roblox 2022-09 3:32](https://youtu.be/F0BteXySO_A?t=212))
- "You need your code to be like a SpaceX rocket; it needs to launch and it needs to fail fast." ([B Ricey 2022-09 6:09](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=369))
- "By prototyping, you ensure that you detect design flaws, oversights and unexpected technical requirements in the early stages of pre-production" ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 0:19](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=19))
- "Prototyping should be fast and quick, hitting certain aspects of the feature being tested but not be the entire feature itself." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 0:50](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=50))
- "Don't create new systems for content cadence. It's just way too much work. You can introduce way too many bugs to your game." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 4:34](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=274))
- "This is very similar to TikTok in a way, but it's definitely not like games on mobile or AAA where the cycles are much smaller slower." ([Roblox 2023-09 28:06](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1686))
- "there's so many errors I still have to fix in the game" ([TheDankDeveloper 2023-10 2:56](https://youtu.be/mwu3k4nXYZQ?t=176))
- "develop means to work on something gradually like literally that's what it means so why are you trying to make your entire game all at once" ([SmartyRBX 2023-10 4:24](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=264))
- "this was basically our MVP people played it people kind of liked it and we improved the monetization largely we improved the gameplay" ([SmartyRBX 2023-10 17:39](https://youtu.be/vemqKJ-5yRE?t=1059))
- "it's very hard most people try to make too large of a game at first and that is the wrong way to go about it" ([SmartyRBX 2023-10 23:50](https://youtu.be/vemqKJ-5yRE?t=1430))
- "I had a scriptor help me with this cuz I'm not the best at scripting" ([PolarBear 2024-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/Q-Wbi1an-ww?t=0))
- "you just got to experiment with some things until something just looks good" ([janisjanis01 2024-03 2:56](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=176))
- "The play accounts are slowly decreasing so I think it's time to completely overhaul it with new maps and features." ([Mulch 2024-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=0))
- "Assistant actually did 100% of all the scripts in that demo and most of the environment building and mundane tasks." ([Roblox 2024-09 12:45](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=765))
- "it's way better to stay on top of performance and optimization as you work on your project as opposed to making it a process that you try and do at the end" ([Roblox 2024-09 3:58](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=238))
- "make simple Games first... don't even think about making your dream projects first until you make 10 to 20 small projects" ([SmartyRBX 2024-09 2:15](https://youtu.be/59AbBgxhfTA?t=135))
- "I think I just got to change a few things and I'm done." ([TheDankDeveloper 2024-10 4:25](https://youtu.be/F2vhC3AuZyk?t=265))
- "pick one skill and just start... make lots and lots and lots of simple creations" ([SmartyRBX 2024-11 1:15](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=75))
- "It was hard to make the game feel balanced with just these backpacks." ([janisjanis01 2025-01 14:53](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=893))
- "You don't technically need these... one way is usually just objectively the easiest and the best one and so that's what developers use." ([ByteBlox 2025-01 6:32](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=392))
- "Until AI is able to fully do that flawlessly under seconds, I think we're going to be fine." ([ByteBlox 2025-01 24:50](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1490))
- "What matters most in the Roblox Market are results: players and Robux, not passion." ([SmartyRBX 2025-03 23:07](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1387))
- "A lot of the stuff in this game is just free models I found and put together so they're reworking a lot of things" ([CallMeDogey 2025-03 12:54](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=774))
- "It's better to create 10 games in a year if you can rather than working on just one game for an entire year, especially if it's risky and unproven." ([AlvinBlox 2025-04 6:37](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=397))
- "Trying to learn as much as you can without doing anything is not the way to go." ([JustDucksey 2025-04 0:05](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=5))
- "After each video I watched, I made something with what I learned." ([JustDucksey 2025-04 0:47](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=47))
- "Done is better than perfect. Ship it, test it, improve it. That's the process." ([AlvinBlox 2025-04 9:33](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=573))
- "Done is better than perfect. Ship it, test it, improve it." ([AlvinBlox 2025-04 9:33](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=573))
- "Make simple games first and then as you gain skill and funds from these games increase the complexity over time of your projects." ([SmartyRBX 2025-06 9:31](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=571))
- "Done is better than perfect." ([SmartyRBX 2025-06 8:07](https://youtu.be/MAnvGNgBKgQ?t=487))
- "Iterate, learn from feedback, from reality rather than working on a game that takes 6 months or 3 years to release and probably never finish it." ([SmartyRBX 2025-07 8:41](https://youtu.be/ddfqOQ7H_h8?t=521))
- "Smart devs make simple games first to get ahead in the Roblox economy." ([SmartyRBX 2025-07 20:43](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=1243))
- "Make simple games first, which is strategy first." ([SmartyRBX 2025-07 5:48](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=348))
- "Roblox projects fall apart as they grow, not because of bad code, but because of that structure." ([Leif 2025-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=0))
- "Organize early to avoid chaos later." ([Leif 2025-08 10:43](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=643))
- "Making a game in 7 days is very hard." ([Erthlord 2025-09 5:25](https://youtu.be/fdDscQ6CWIs?t=325))
- "Multiversal Delivery is actually their first ever finished game." ([Duckable 2025-10 7:35](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=455))
- "They had to take part. So they got to work, spending an entire day just to get the arms to function" ([Duckable 2025-10 6:35](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=395))
- "I prefer to do our own original spin on it." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-10 1:36:35](https://youtu.be/XtWTnz56ZpM?t=5795))
- "Speed is key. You're better off releasing something that's unfinished and unpolished and trying it out." ([VuukStudios 2025-11 5:03](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=303))
- "Which, spoiler, it probably wasn't." ([DitchyDevelopments 2025-11 1:27](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=87))
- "You need to do is make a small game you can beat in one sitting. I jumped straight into a very big pond." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-11 31:07](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=1867))
- "I rushed releasing it and in the process didn't realize that it had multiple problems." ([janisjanis01 2025-11 16:47](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=1007))
- "Our current systems were not able to handle the things that we want. We need to rewrite most of our scripts and the entire item system because the whole game was built around items." ([Lnor Roblox 2025-12 3:59](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=239))
- "Roblox as a platform moves very quickly. So you have to be fast with the creation of games and the release of games." ([Daiorg 2025-12 44:05](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2645))
- "You can still make something cool with like the limitations." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-12 1:30:07](https://youtu.be/LyJ04iE6K1M?t=5407))
- "This idea lasted 3 days before I decided I wanted to add Parkour!" ([DitchyDevelopments 2026-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=0))
- "make sure that your game is fun before you start adding stuff to it." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-03 48:11](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=2891))
- "we struggled to update it cuz the scripting in the game was hard" ([1_Bruce 2026-03 1:16](https://youtu.be/zgq6L4ZzCHs?t=76))
- "Have the framework and the groundwork for it come first and then build all your details on top." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-03 1:04:56](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3896))
- "At your stage in development, speed is way more important than polish and no bugs." ([rasdev 2026-05 3:18](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=198))
- "It's not this linear process, it's this curved road. There's forks in it all over." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-05 16:01](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=961))
- "When you give that release date, it's based off what you have at that point in time; things come up you couldn't have calculated for." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-05 17:23](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=1043))
- "Premature optimization is the root of evil. ... you should not be obsessed over efficiency." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258))
- "Once you have your game design document, this is kind of like the scope of the project." ([Tizzy 2026-05 11:38](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=698))
- "Just launch and see how players respond, because the quicker you can get live players in there, the quicker you can get data." ([Tizzy 2026-05 14:23](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=863))
- "I spent months on a project that I ended up scrapping. So, I wanted to make something really, really quick." ([Roblox 2026-05 0:44](https://youtu.be/-14vhiLL4xE?t=44))
- "Simple games usually are the best." ([rasdev 2026-06 4:05](https://youtu.be/q0uePCbBlk0?t=245))
- "Do it every single day and you will get better. There is no getting worse at it." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-06 1:13:19](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=4399))
- "A tiny finished game with 12 players is worth more than a massive unfinished game with zero." ([ArTheDev 2026-06 2:43](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=163))
- "He updated daily, sometimes twice a day, and he followed the data." ([Tizzy 2026-06 0:42](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=42))
- "99% of developers think they can get something done quicker than they truly can." ([Yann 2026-06 2:03](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=123))
- "Don't ever just try to wait for the perfect moment or make the perfect game." ([rasdev 2026-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=246))
- "Fable 5 is getting to the point where that's basically not a problem anymore. You still need to understand game design." ([MoseyTube 2026-07 4:07](https://youtu.be/1Oicl2fFt3o?t=247))
- "Sell Potatoes didn't make $43,292 because I built the perfect monetization setup on day one." ([MoseyTube 2026-07 5:11](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=311))
- "Don't make a game, make the pieces. The game finds itself later." ([waveshine 2026-07 5:47](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=347))
- "If touching one line breaks everything and you don't know why, you didn't learn anything." ([waveshine 2026-07 6:28](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=388))
- "As long as your game's core loop is playable, release it." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 3:07](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=187))
- "It's better to work 1 hour a day at least on your game than hopping on twice a week for 7 hours straight." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 7:13](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=433))
- "Get product in front of people and learn cheap lessons. The faster we get something in front of an audience, the quicker we can learn." ([Business of Creators 2026-08 10:30](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=630))
- "The first 7 to 14 days after you publish your game are your make or break." ([MoseyTube 2026-08 5:41](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=341))
- "You do not finish a game by releasing it. The actual work starts once you release the game." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 3:25](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=205))
- "I've been able to crack out slop in 1 week, hell, even 1 day, which makes spending even just 10 days working on one game feel like an eternity." ([Duckable 2026-08 16:31](https://youtu.be/3XX3Y74hzq0?t=991))
- "Watch the videos. Don't just follow them along. Don't watch them and not actually be in studio. You need to be in studio for it to work." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 2:06](https://youtu.be/7cRCq-sJa_Q?t=126))
- "There's one thing that you must do before building a Roblox game... simply just plan out your game." ([RoyalDev 2026-09 0:02](https://youtu.be/MZ-nsDEVXH4?t=2))
- "So 14 months later, they replaced it with one rebuilt from scratch live with millions of people already playing it." ([Roblox 2026-09 38:46](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=2326))
- "I literally made this game in like 3 to 4 days or something... it was getting like zero players. It was completely dead." ([kra_DeZ 2026-09 1:34](https://youtu.be/ZradA-cygP0?t=94))
- "Just seeing your game on paper before you begin helps you get it done so much better, so much cleaner." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 3:26](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=206))
- "Just seeing the date, having a fixed date on something too is like really makes you want to do it and complete it more." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 5:19](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=319))
- "Testing proved it made controls clumsy and ruined the high-speed tempo, so we cut it immediately." ([mats-dept2 2026-10 1:13](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=73))
- "Use it as your work... spot dumb things as you're going." ([Roblox 2026-10 4:44](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=284))
- "It's a lot of work, but it's totally worth it." ([Roblox 2026-10 16:57](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1017))
- "Effort that you're about to put in to building the game is not a tax. That's what makes it yours." ([Roblox 2026-10 28:18](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1698))
- "Don't spend a year working on a game that's not proven; at least get players in and get some data back." ([Roblox 2026-10 15:51](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=951))
- "never try to make a game in one prompt. You don't want to let AI do everything for you. You want to use it as a tool." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=40))
- "90% of the success that comes from your game is the planning that happened beforehand." ([rasdev 2026-10 7:16](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=436))
- "They're just hype just for quick money grabs and then you move on to the next game." ([kra_DeZ 2026-10 10:56](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=656))
- "All games support multiplayer out of the gate. No need to write complicated backend code, no need to set up or pay for your own servers." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox))
- "Assets are automatically moderated, so you can modify and publish your game immediately across console, desktop, and mobile devices." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox))
- "Don't wait until it's too late to pivot." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies))
- "Make sure the gameplay feels good before fleshing out a map and realizing that you wasted all your time." ([Roblox Creators 2026-10 32:01](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=1921))
- "GAME IS IN ALPHA, EXPECT BUGS AND CHANGES" ([Roblox public API 2026-11 ](https://www.roblox.com/games/93978595733734/))

## [[community-and-discord]]
- "Don't just pick your friends to be on the team. Just because you're friends with them doesn't mean they're suited to be a leader." ([Last Level Studios 2019-09 16:06](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=966))
- "When you can't figure it out, the first step should not be to ask for help. Go to the dev wiki, go to stackoverflow, go to the dev forums." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618))
- "you actually get a lot of value from open sourcing at least part of your code in your game... you're helping new developers" ([Last Level Studios 2020-03 29:58](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=1798))
- "I'm definitely one of the people who supports having the dev forum be more open." ([Last Level Studios 2020-03 9:34](https://youtu.be/n0ODJH9EgWc?t=574))
- "When you engage with them, they engage back and you build that mutual relationship that encourages them to stay in your game." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 46:46](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2806))
- "Without understanding your consumers, predicting what people would want would be essentially a shot in the dark." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 2:07](https://youtu.be/wrf6DRlfoO8?t=127))
- "Trolls just want to provoke you and your community into displaying negative emotions." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 19:17](https://youtu.be/wrf6DRlfoO8?t=1157))
- "I'm a big believer in rising flutter whatever lifts all boats." ([Last Level Studios 2022-01 6:58](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=418))
- "Fifteen minutes don't go by without somebody complaining about exploiters." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356))
- "it's really important that you try and give it as much information as possible" ([GnomeCode 2022-09 20:18](https://youtu.be/aFKaY-PmR08?t=1218))
- "we've actually onboarded quite a few people from that realm of the community to start helping us make games" ([Roblox Creators 2023-06 1:29](https://youtu.be/MW-QvpIxO4I?t=89))
- "Communities are going to be more forgiving and understanding when you do trip up, because they know the people behind the screen." ([Roblox Creators 2023-08 8:02](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=482))
- "they invited their friends, then their friends, then their friends, etc… Eventually, your game will reach a point of stable player counts." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279))
- "it's very difficult to attract the initial users if you don't have the community support" ([Roblox Creators 2024-08 23:34](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1414))
- "this Niche is severely untapped there's not that much competition but not for long" ([SmartyRBX 2024-09 6:50](https://youtu.be/59AbBgxhfTA?t=410))
- "join some Discord servers... get to know other people because those other people will help you" ([SmartyRBX 2024-11 4:02](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=242))
- "thanks to everyone who's checked out the game so far... there's actually been quite a bit of enthusiasm for this thing" ([GnomeCode 2025-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/OyPgQgBSIe4?t=0))
- "For years, we have had people reaching out to us in our community forums asking if they could get plushies or stickers, so we knew we had a strong audience." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/expanding-how-commerce-can-help-you-earn-and-grow-your-communities/3651181))
- "I think you should make devlogs, but don't publish any of them until the game comes out." ([RoBuilder 2025-06 16:29](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=989))
- "Let's just keep everyone on Roblox and we're going to get very good at that." ([Roblox 2025-07 35:08](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=2108))
- "I think it's more building a culture around you rather than marketing individual items." ([Roblox Creators 2025-08 14:06](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=846))
- "people have 2 days to create an entire game based off a weird theme" ([Duckable 2025-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=0))
- "No matter how the algorithm feels about your game on a given day, you will have those ride or die players that are ready to play your game." ([bubDEV 2025-12 10:57](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=657))
- "DevForum users have bluntly stated that the brainrot wave "absolutely ruined Roblox in 2025."" ([RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02 ](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts))
- "These players, these artists, should start designing creatures for the game. They were the ones playing it." ([Roblox 2026-03 1:07](https://youtu.be/eph8HDbEs6U?t=67))
- "A lot of people started taking us more seriously after the Hunt event." ([Creators on the Blox 2026-03 1:27:40](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=5260))
- "Visualize what it's going to be like for a content creator to actually play the game." ([Tizzy 2026-07 16:30](https://youtu.be/FnsIL4zPCQg?t=990))
- "Kevin, ABD is a passion project... What's the point of having a game if no one's going to play it?" ([Cumin 2026-08 21:41](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1301))
- "Community posts, Twitter/X, and group shout-outs only reach whoever already checks those." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4850631))
- "Similar to trading, it's bad for the economy. Instead, we would rather spend time to fix the anti-che, so it's fair for everyone." ([Vindooly 2026-09 10:07](https://youtu.be/GSrXI-biGuA?t=607))
- "Roblox is more of a social platform than a gaming platform in my opinion." ([Roblox 2026-10 22:57](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=1377))
- "Cultivating a healthy community and involving users in development is key to success." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/how-is-roblox-different))
- "Be sure to focus your application on your domain expertise and what other creators will learn from you." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/spotlights))
- "Because of the prominent role of organic, word-of-mouth traffic for success on Roblox, communicating with users is critical." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/roblox-user-base))

## [[business-and-payouts]]
- "Start out small and utilise the free methods to build players and get initial capital you can then use on marketing." ([Roblox 2018-07 38:01](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=2281))
- "localization is way more than just translation; a lot of people miss that" ([Roblox 2018-07 1:41](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=101))
- "You don't need Premium 2200 to participate in the Developer Exchange program; any level of Premium will work." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-premium-is-here/355721))
- "You can now cash out any amount of Robux above R$100,000 and are no longer restricted to preset amounts!" ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/tier-less-cash-outs-are-here-%F0%9F%8E%89/370137))
- "Going forward, the fee for all developer products and game passes is 30%, regardless of whether or not you have Premium." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/unified-marketplace-fee-for-dev-products-and-game-passes/507109))
- "With Premium Payouts, developers that drive playtime can generate additional revenue." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/increasing-premium-payouts/753309))
- "Robux earned today through Premium Payouts will be increased by over 75%. Your engagement scores may stay the same, but you'll see your projected payouts increase gradually." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/increasing-premium-payouts-starting-today/1072865))
- "Once you reach saturation and face rule changes that make organic growth harder, you get waves where growth only continues through acquisition." ([GameMakers 2021-06 35:07](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=2107))
- "we don't actually get all of this robux because roblox takes about 30 cut of everything that you earn" ([Mulch 2021-09 8:37](https://youtu.be/yG44d_Xnbwg?t=517))
- "A lot of our income goes to donations around the world, and it's the reason we do everything we do." ([Roblox 2022-09 3:29](https://youtu.be/jULYHFTmq4s?t=209))
- "A game counts as commercial the moment it generates any kind of revenue, regardless of if its creator does so purposely or not." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490))
- "the licensing teams of most big brands do the conference circuits." ([Roblox Creators 2023-05 1:26](https://youtu.be/3Y4_B5-EHjY?t=86))
- "The Creator keeps everything else. Roblox does not take anything on top of that." ([Roblox 2023-09 27:24](https://youtu.be/8R136Y_T5G4?t=1644))
- "Focus on making a good game that people will actually buy; use your limited time and money there rather than making a limited liability company." ([Big Turtle 2024-03 1:35](https://youtu.be/n119LjiQ_ns?t=95))
- "we actually lost 2,953 Robux" ([TheDankDeveloper 2024-07 9:39](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=579))
- "You do not really want to accept Robux only. If you're accepting Robux only, you're going to have to find another payment method." ([VuukStudios 2024-07 4:42](https://youtu.be/NfXvHlAdIsI?t=282))
- "whenever you get 30,000 Robux you can cash out for like $100 etc" ([VuukStudios 2024-08 5:13](https://youtu.be/3J7Vp6NgYf4?t=313))
- "Never give someone a percent of your game in exchange for advertising. It is 100% a scam." ([CallMeDogey 2025-05 1:20](https://youtu.be/ZAfE-Row9tU?t=80))
- "They only care when they make money. That's it." ([CallMeDogey 2025-05 4:03](https://youtu.be/ZAfE-Row9tU?t=243))
- "Personally, I've made over 3 million Robux through commissions." ([Arow 2025-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/JeBDqH7INjY?t=0))
- "I make thousands a month from dev content. I don't make thousands a month from games." ([SmartyRBX 2025-06 9:14](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=554))
- "a developer basically only takes home about 21%" ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 1:27](https://youtu.be/LRyThu3bFyg?t=87))
- "Why only 7 days? ... It's really important to make that decision in a timely way." ([Roblox 2025-09 25:21](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=1521))
- "I might be down over 400 bucks in Robux, but I don't consider it as a loss. As long as you try, you can never be a loser." ([janisjanis01 2025-11 17:53](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=1073))
- "If we did a GoFundMe and we didn't fully fund the event, they would be expecting us to just start working with not enough money." ([Creators on the Blox 2025-11 1:30:37](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=5437))
- "Roblox pays you roughly 0.0038 per Robux, so that 6,500 Robux translates to roughly $21 in real spendable cash." ([Matt The Method 2025-12 2:41](https://youtu.be/MPHPumDvMIs?t=161))
- "A lot of people talking about UGC are not making money from UGC. They're making money selling courses about UGC." ([Britt Drake 2025-12 9:45](https://youtu.be/ncZn8_hopuc?t=585))
- "It's a capital P platform of interactive experiences aimed at maximizing revenue and brand retention." ([Izzzyzzz 2026-01 46:42](https://youtu.be/KIQzaUhZW-w?t=2802))
- "If you have ever had a game reach 100 plus active players, it's likely you've received a message like this, where someone asks to buy your game outright." ([CallMeDogey 2026-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/IQm6zE2fKUI?t=0))
- "When a customer buys your asset, the money from the sale will enter a 30-day escrow period." ([Roblox Creators 2026-04 2:46](https://youtu.be/NAwQr9sTy74?t=166))
- "Robux you bought with real money, or got as a gift, can never go through DevEx. Not ever." ([Generalist Programmer 2026-07 2:39](https://youtu.be/9mV423C2aXU?t=159))
- "Many groups ask for your game's analytics, then take the data to help their own games. Some deals are the worst mistakes you can make." ([Duckable 2026-07 17:05](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=1025))
- "Treat the owner account as the single key to the vault." ([Generalist Programmer 2026-07 3:03](https://youtu.be/XzxoIlpBnJA?t=183))
- "Roblox is not deducting any of your earnings." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-regarding-group-and-account-balance-corrections/4756316))
- "If the game didn't succeed, we wouldn't have the money probably to pay the MG... it would have been the final thing that just literally bankrupted us." ([Business of Creators 2026-08 5:40](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=340))
- "We have a total profit of minus 170,734 Robux. This is still great because we got a game with over 100 players." ([janisjanis01 2026-08 19:06](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1146))
- "Roblox needs to have 30,000 Robux before it even lets you try to withdraw money and I am at the 1% of that total." ([MonarchDev 2026-09 4:05](https://youtu.be/LD7LGP10dRc?t=245))
- "Your earnings will be paid out automatically every business day in USD. There's no request to submit and no monthly cooldown." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505))
- "There's no request to submit and no monthly cooldown. Transfer to your bank account on your schedule." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505))
- "We'll transition away from DevEx in mid-2027 in the US. DevEx will remain longer in other regions." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880))
- "We have an incredible DevEx system today; so many creators are benefiting from it and earning every single year, and it's only growing." ([GamesBeat 2026-09 ](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-makes-a-play-for-triple-a-developers-at-rdc-2026/))
- "It is hard for new creators to really understand how it all works... Wallet is just a really intuitive product that I think creators have needed for a long time." ([GamesBeat 2026-09 ](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-makes-a-play-for-triple-a-developers-at-rdc-2026/))
- "This is a game of numbers and a game of speed. Don't be salty that someone else literally was faster than you." ([VuukStudios 2026-09 3:33](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=213))
- "What creators receive in real money is determined by Roblox in its sole discretion." ([Where The Money Went 2026-10 12:16](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=736))
- "The existence of the DevEx Program today in no way creates an obligation for us to continue the program in the future." ([Where The Money Went 2026-10 12:58](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=778))
- "Roblox acts as the principal distributor and merchant of record for purchases made through Roblox. Users and Creators do not enter into a direct transaction with each other." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/updates-to-the-roblox-terms-of-use-november-2026/4907620))
- "If you want to be like these guys and create viral Roblox game ideas that can get you millions of dollars, you can check out Reblox." ([rasdev 2026-10 4:49](https://youtu.be/Weui21kVEIY?t=289))
- "A company came... we want to buy your game. And he sold that game immediately." ([rasdev 2026-10 3:05](https://youtu.be/4GEHEzhvhwI?t=185))
- "Licensing is now self-serve on Roblox. Open the license manager on Creator Hub, submit your request, and once approved, you're ready to go." ([Roblox 2026-10 8:07](https://youtu.be/oxiaIhN5Ybo?t=487))
- "none of these games have actually said how much they've made. Everything is just calculations" ([rasdev 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=0))
- "you definitely need at least 30,000 in your account, and you have to have like an email verified" ([Xaiyne 2026-10 1:23](https://youtu.be/7-b69FYDd5k?t=83))
- "Roblox offers a higher exchange rate of 0.0054 for certain Earned Robux in eligible games from purchases from U.S. players who have verified their age as at least 18." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-exchange))

## [[common-mistakes]]
- "Call THIS ONCE IN THE ENTIRE GAME!!!!!!!" ([Roblox DevForum 2017-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-gameanalytics-retention-and-monetisation-ez-mode/41083))
- "If you've bait[ed] players with an advert, how are they going to trust you that their products are going to be what they expect." ([Roblox 2018-07 22:51](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1371))
- "If you fork the player scripts to do that, there is now an Xbox-shaped hole in your total addressable market." ([Roblox 2018-07 3:59](https://youtu.be/a2DaoXERzhE?t=239))
- "A computer can't pull off stunts like this for you; without open communication with translators, they won't rewrite content." ([Roblox 2018-07 15:18](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=918))
- "Roblox has recently announced their plan to remove support for PointsService on June 30th." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754))
- "Anything your community does often reflects on you, even if it's against your rules and you don't condone it." ([Last Level Studios 2019-09 6:17](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=377))
- "Unlike in vanilla lua, it is impossible to force a garbage collection." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954))
- "Before you blame humanoids for your game's lackluster performance, analyze your code." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801))
- "the feature games would get lucky if they've reached a hundred players" ([anomalousDucky 2020-01 2:44](https://youtu.be/R5LnkhhJtno?t=164))
- "asking for help on the forum sucks because the first guy replies says something really dumb and it's right there at the top forever" ([Last Level Studios 2020-03 34:07](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=2047))
- "Splitting the hunt across 48 games with no hub and paid passes caused heavy criticism of Roblox and developers." ([ThatGuyEvan 2020-04 11:52](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=712))
- "I'm no longer interested in tycoons... test the games a lot more thoroughly right before agreeing to a deal." ([TheDevKing 2020-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=617))
- "Anchor things that shouldn't move! This makes Roblox calculate more physics, thus causing beautiful lag." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842))
- "never have a window that opens up with no content in it because all that's gonna do the first time people play your game they're gonna see the button" ([GnomeCode 2021-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=41))
- "We weren't really monitoring how long it was actually taking us to make and we weren't evaluating where a lot of our resources were going." ([Roblox Creators 2021-07 52:46](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3166))
- "Make rules for your games that state that ableism is not allowed." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919))
- "What I would recommend is you go with the most difficult platform to make UI on, which is phones, and then you just sort of scale up." ([Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:10:01](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=4201))
- "Newer developers tend to over complicate the game." ([Roblox Creators 2021-11 20:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1244))
- "Do not form a company partnership. I cannot stress this enough. Do not do it." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 4:34](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=274))
- "Industry content ratings are static; with dynamic guidelines, the same experience can be suitable for all ages and regions." ([Roblox Creators 2021-12 15:10](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=910))
- "Nobody's going in thinking I can't wait to experience this story; they go in wanting to play with friends." ([Last Level Studios 2021-12 31:21](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=1881))
- "You've got this dream... you don't think about how they're going to combine into a game." ([Last Level Studios 2022-01 24:10](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1450))
- "They'll try and present you with a minimum guarantee, what they'll call an MG." ([Roblox Creators 2022-05 34:10](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=2050))
- "Generally speaking, you should not focus on monetization if your game has not gained much traction." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688))
- "What is your core loop? Get your core loop up early and think about how all of your systems work with it." ([Roblox Creators 2022-06 52:31](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=3151))
- "if you don't have players and they're not engaged in your game you get nowhere" ([Roblox Creators 2022-06 32:20](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=1940))
