## [[ui-ux-design]]
- "I make my UIs 100% in Scale unless I have a specific need for offset. All three of my recent games have had only a single UI that scales perfectly across all devices." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461))
- "Offset is not very good either, and for support on multiple devices will require redesigning your UI multiple times." ([Roblox DevForum 2017-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461))
- "Anything on the player's screen should only be there to inform the Player about things that they need to know that relate to the game." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258))
- "Some of my games don't have any button objects at all, it's all just Frames, TextLabels and ImageLabels." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948))
- "It's just not worth trying to mess around with an object that doesn't quite get exactly what you want where you could just make it yourself." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948))
- "Marking your entire game with an epilepsy warning means that people with epilepsy will be forced to choose between their safety and trying to play your game anyway." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919))
- "A golden rule of design is to never put pure white text on black backgrounds and vice versa." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805))
- "UI that has consistency throughout tends to be received better and appears better." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805))
- "The reason I feel this is appealing and clean is because it's simple and minimalistic." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083))
- "Such as UI corners so there are no icky corners." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083))
- "make sure that if you were a new player that you could easily understand what is going on" ([ByteBlox 2024-07 16:06](https://youtu.be/ZTzUHfMeYKA?t=966))
- "A lot of Roblox games seemingly don't have progress bars; in something like an obby or tycoon a progress bar would do wonders." ([ByteBlox 2024-09 27:28](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=1648))
- "please disable the force field of the spawn location so many games they have the little force field that appears when you load in and that breaks immersion" ([GnomeCode 2024-10 10:30](https://youtu.be/H9CbgCe47Mo?t=630))
- "Roblox never tells you to use scale; it lets you do whatever, and a lot of people don't test on phone." ([ByteBlox 2024-11 14:10](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=850))
- "every time you join the game it'll pick a random background out of 11 Unique Images" ([janisjanis01 2024-11 21:12](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=1272))
- "Scale is a fraction of the parent, from zero to one. Offset is a flat number of pixels added on top." ([Generalist Programmer 2026-07 2:47](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=167))
- "Anything you put in StarterGui gets copied into each player's PlayerGui when they join." ([Generalist Programmer 2026-07 0:59](https://youtu.be/BJea3LJJWww?t=59))
- "Import your Aseprite files in one click, then bring them to life in your game with a new performant animation API." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734))
- "Very few educators understand common gamer terms like W-A-S-D but everyone knows arrow keys on a keyboard." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings))

## [[sound-and-music]]
- "These changes require that you make use of the Audio Discovery plugin in Studio to review the ownership of all audio assets in your experience." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1715717))
- "March 22nd is a hard deadline." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1715717))
- "We always wanted there to be a more intriguing sound in the distance no matter where the player is located in the house." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/design-dark-soundscapes))
- "Sounds that directly contribute to player progression were given priority over sounds that don't, such as wind, rain, footsteps, and atmosphere." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/design-dark-soundscapes))

## [[optimization-and-streaming]]
- "Please please please do not do this! Using a loop and updating the position of each individual crate over 30 times a second." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895))
- "Greedy meshing is a really useful concept when working with voxels, especially in Roblox where large numbers of parts can cause a lot of lag." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717))
- "You can always expect to see better, or at least equal performance by coalescing things together into bigger chunks. You'll never see worse overall performance." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078))
- "Make sure to set all your vines, mushrooms, grass, and small plant material to off! This will have great FPS benefits in the long run!" ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419))
- "Every shadow costs resources to render." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419))
- "Use partial object Transparency sparingly, as semi-transparent objects inhibit a lot of internal rendering optimizations." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842))
- "You should just program with streaming enabled on even if you're not going to leave it on for the final games." ([Last Level Studios 2022-01 30:01](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1801))
- "In a big map you probably don't need to check every part if it's touching or not." ([BubuTheDev 2025-06 5:01](https://youtu.be/0UYtSJvYe4U?t=301))
- "If you don't have your transparency on zero or one, it will lag your game." ([BubuTheDev 2025-06 5:47](https://youtu.be/0UYtSJvYe4U?t=347))
- "Fake it if you can't make it. Only these bullets are purely visual. The server simulates them." ([Roox4 2025-06 2:46](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=166))
- "Doublesided does not actually affect a game's performance." ([RoBuilder 2025-06 1:20:59](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=4859))
- "SLIM is some sort of cloud technology that happens in the background; the algorithms run on Roblox's servers." ([Last Level Studios 2025-11 11:16](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=676))
- "The rule of thumb is you should only send necessary data, when it is necessary." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236))
- "Pushing as much load away from the server and leaving it up to the clients." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236))
- "Players lost due to performance issues translate to less revenue to you." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258))
- "You want to deparent things if you don't want them to render at the moment." ([Roblox 2026-10 17:34](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=1054))

## [[testing-debugging-and-qa]]
- "You can't really get the game pass in Roblox Studio, we're going to have to try it out with Roblox player." ([SimTek Game Development 2023-08 14:08](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=848))
- "sometimes fixing the bugs is as much about finding how to recreate them" ([GnomeCode 2025-02 8:59](https://youtu.be/OyPgQgBSIe4?t=539))
- "If you're starting to run automatic iterations every night, and you need to do a test iteration between them, there's no way to do that unless everything is automated." ([Roblox 2026-04 31:48](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=1908))

## [[version-control-and-tooling]]
- "Merge features into main, rebase main into features." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661))
- "you cannot have multiple sessions working on the same thing at the same time simply because they'll clash one another" ([MonarchDev 2026-10 5:20](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=320))

## [[trailers-and-video-marketing]]
- "There's a lot of people who want to watch dev content, but there are very few making it." ([SmartyRBX 2025-02 3:18](https://youtu.be/Da83Ft52DLQ?t=198))
- "I only posted for 3 days, which is barely anything, and still saw decent results." ([janisjanis01 2025-05 8:15](https://youtu.be/sM9btPAvb28?t=495))
- "First of all, you need to have a hokey intro, great thumbnail, and looped video. That's about it." ([janisjanis01 2025-06 9:41](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=581))
- "Throughout these days, I have realized that maybe it's better to make higher quality content than pumping out a bunch of videos." ([janisjanis01 2025-06 8:39](https://youtu.be/5LMGVfslAYs?t=519))
- "I made ads on TikTok and Pinterest posting two to three times a week minimum. And from there, I was just being consistent with clothing and ads." ([#7Phantom 2025-12 1:43](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=103))
- "I figured out that I had to milk the set along with my other best-selling clothing if I wanted to keep up the momentum." ([#7Phantom 2025-12 0:59](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=59))
- "Even the best trailer won't save your game if it's trash, and it can't manufacture fun." ([rasdev 2026-08 10:10](https://youtu.be/MH9TaOMTm9Q?t=610))
- "We believe the video gave potential players a better idea of what the game actually had to offer before deciding to click and play" ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gameplay-videos-on-home-help-your-games-get-discovered/4842601))
- "Lo-fi organic storytelling and developer diaries drive top-rated requirements on short form like TikTok." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 14:55](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=895))

## [[influencer-and-creator-marketing]]
- "you need to give them a reason to play your game" ([TheDevBacon 2024-07 0:40](https://youtu.be/_XJVDRr2ZuA?t=40))
- "Provide screenshots, provide trailers, provide a link to your game. YouTubers should just be able to see what the game is all about, and be able to play it right then and there." ([Cumin 2026-02 0:41](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=41))
- "The winning strategies are the ones that have influencer marketing as a permanent pillar, not a seasonal add-on." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 4:47](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=287))
- "The winning strategies are the ones that have been having it as a permanent pillar and not as just a seasonal add-on." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 1:28](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=88))
- "The thing is that it should not be competing with paid UA. It just makes it more efficient." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 2:49](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=169))

## [[ugc-and-avatar-economy]]
- "For now, creators cannot release Limited, Limited Unique, or resellable UGC items. This is something we hope to open up as the UGC catalog becomes available to more creators." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ugc-catalog-is-now-live/331405))
- "The purpose of this change is to discourage item upload abuse and we hope that this will lead to a better Shop experience for both shoppers and creators." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/upcoming-changes-to-avatar-catalog-upload-requirements/915265))
- "every time somebody purchases this, I get 30% because of Roblox commissions." ([Mulch 2022-07 2:06](https://youtu.be/An_5Mu0aR5I?t=126))
- "they said it was going to go limited and the items never went limited so a ton of people were buying these items" ([ThatGuyEvan 2022-08 36:35](https://youtu.be/APeSOO8WRPQ?t=2195))
- "I'd just do like cash grabby stuff like what I've done. That's seems to do pretty well." ([Arow 2025-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/yM28oinpQIo?t=0))
- "You could just buy a knockoff that some guy made in 2 minutes in Blender." ([Cumin 2025-11 4:20](https://youtu.be/66n0P2NtBoQ?t=260))
- "We launched Avatar backgrounds a few weeks ago to let players personalize their profile pages, and now we're opening creation up to you." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pilot-create-and-sell-avatar-backgrounds-on-marketplace/4778993))
- "I've made most of my Robux through UGC, just uploading them and stuff." ([Xaiyne 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/7-b69FYDd5k?t=0))
- "Avatar assets created in-experience are not listed on the Marketplace, and cannot be resold or traded." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation))
- "the more original and creative your design is, the more its design elements are protected by copyright" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property))

## [[localization-and-international]]
- "If you have better options use them. Some players do notice, and do you appreciate it." ([ContextLost 2018-03 2:43](https://youtu.be/wKjTYdVi4KE?t=163))
- "If you stop at throwing in some quick translations and think you're done, you can't expect automatic success." ([ContextLost 2018-03 4:47](https://youtu.be/wKjTYdVi4KE?t=287))
- "We will always give developers the choice of which countries their games are published in." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/about-our-upcoming-global-compliance-system/461447))
- "We will only be enforcing local compliance in those local markets." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-02 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/about-our-upcoming-global-compliance-system/461447))
- "automatic translations will always be additive so we would never override any human translations that you have in place" ([TanookiAlex 2021-02 6:06](https://youtu.be/ZEQmRsvDIAg?t=366))
- "if you are the developer that has the ability to hire your translators for your game we encourage you to do so" ([TanookiAlex 2021-02 8:48](https://youtu.be/ZEQmRsvDIAg?t=528))
- "You don't have to support every language; you can translate as many as you want, and that control is the beauty of it." ([Last Level Studios 2021-07 10:24](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=624))
- "If you really want translations just hire someone; enable automatic translations only short-term while you correct them." ([Last Level Studios 2021-07 14:28](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=868))
- "Auto Translation seems to be overriding manual translations again. It doesn't override the saved string in the localization table on the website, only what's displayed in-game." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/auto-translation-overrides-manual-translations/4958947))
- "Roblox does not recommend or endorse any particular translator or Job Creator, or her/his/its respective offered services or job posts." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/notable-translators))

## [[age-ratings-moderation-and-tos]]
- "This is a hard problem to solve, and we're still working on a solution that works at a large scale." ([Roblox DevForum 2019-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ugc-catalog-is-now-live/331405))
- "Creators in the UGC Program must adhere to our Community Standards, Terms of Use, and Marketplace Requirements which prohibit creating items that are overly similar to existing items." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-03 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/taking-action-on-copies-of-roblox-created-limiteds/2226390))
- "Those who have verified that they're at least 17 years old with a selfie and a photo of their government-issued ID will be able to create or access these experiences." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184))
- "Simulated gambling will no longer be allowed on our platform." ([Roblox DevForum 2023-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184))
- "Because Roblox does not appear to enforce its own advertising standards and has reportedly taken the position that branded virtual worlds on its platform doesn't constitute advertising." ([Izzzyzzz 2026-01 21:49](https://youtu.be/KIQzaUhZW-w?t=1309))
- "don't make them look sus. I've had a few taken down... Roblox decided that twerking probably isn't the best emote to have on their platform." ([Xaiyne 2026-03 7:33](https://youtu.be/O5FjkMlRPPI?t=453))
- "If your game is like a horror 16+ game... do not pay this because there's a high chance they're going to deny like cuz they're just looking for things to deny." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 4:13](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=253))
- "It disproportionately benefits studios and actively hurts new creators. It is time to remove it completely." ([Pwnedbloxx 2026-08 6:47](https://youtu.be/83rA23MDGqc?t=407))
- "Rewarding users to consume a continuous feed of content without natural stopping points is not appropriate for younger users." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-new-restrictions-on-reward-driven-media-feeds/4829188))
- "If the average user believes consuming the media feed is a requirement for being rewarded, this policy applies." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-new-restrictions-on-reward-driven-media-feeds/4829188))
- "if your game contains no age-restricted content, you simply answer one confirmation question to immediately publish to players ages 16 and older in most regions." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-simpler-path-to-publish-plus-invite-early-testers/4930524))
- "Age groups 16-17 and 21+ cannot talk with each other, but can, and almost always play games with each other." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/age-based-chat-restrictions-changes/4962382))
- "Preset systems must reinforce existing Roblox safety features – not work around them." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines))
- "any content you create must respect the intellectual property rights of others – both on and off our platform" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property))

## [[studio-business-funding-and-publishing]]
- "You want to make them believe that your game will do good." ([TheDevKing 2020-04 6:07](https://youtu.be/VOKFE8Qi16c?t=367))
- "Investors want to invest into games that have a future, games that have true potential." ([The Letus Garden 2020-11 2:07](https://youtu.be/pAfbzNESkYk?t=127))
- "Investors still want to see organic growth; they want to see teams that you're spending money against build." ([GameMakers 2021-06 36:28](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=2188))
- "The challenge with earlier-stage companies is you don't have the history of success and data to deploy capital with confidence." ([GameMakers 2021-06 29:17](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=1757))
- "You don't need to have a good product, you need to have users. You need to have audience. You need to have community." ([Naavik Gaming Podcast 2026-03 35:01](https://youtu.be/mykMEsgoi5E?t=2101))
- "Jumpstart support is primarily in-kind: guidance, Robux-based operational support, and visibility tools, not a salary or traditional publishing deal." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/jumpstart))

## [[brand-deals-and-ip-licensing]]
- "We've certainly know of developers that have taken brand sponsorships and actually made less revenue than they would have done if they just left it alone." ([Ventures 2025-12 21:27](https://youtu.be/Nb50UhXcpRM?t=1287))
- "As creators are maturing, they're discovering the benefits that come with owning their own intellectual property, like expanding into merchandising and more traditional media." ([GamesBeat 2026-02 ](https://www.gamesbeat.com/roblox-creators-view-film-and-tv-adaptations-as-a-promising-revenue-stream/))
- "Starting today, you can use licensed IP in your game that's tied to specific game pass and developer product sales, instead of to the entire game." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/licensing-platform-update-in-game-sales-license-and-3-new-ko/4868288))

## [[player-psychology-and-game-feel]]
- "The bobbing shouldn't be a 10 stud difference; that can easily cause players to leave your game." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943))
- "try not to punish too harshly - i.e. don't over-punish beyond what would happen if they lost the battle." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912))
- "I want to teach a lesson that generally, in real life, arguments are usually made through not seeing through someone's perspective." ([Emancyphur 2020-09 10:36](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=636))
- "To be honest, there are barely any games that use camera animations. Even though a good camera can make an attack feel much more powerful." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-04 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/camera-animations-for-cutscenes-and-gameplay/1181691))
- "the audience sort of recognizes that they're not being manipulated... they react a little more kindly to their participation in the game" ([GameMakers 2022-11 40:41](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2441))
- "You need to make your game look and feel so fun that Albert's friends won't pull out their phone and scroll through TikTok." ([1Coal 2023-08 1:04](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=64))
- "If the players are being treated like a wallet, it's a very hard battle to win; if they're having fun they'll happily spend money." ([GameMakers 2024-03 20:36](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=1236))
- "I think we're good on the 10% aspect however I think it's changing too slowly maybe 5 seconds is a little too long" ([ByteBlox 2024-03 8:52](https://youtu.be/lJ_ZwfNHkQU?t=532))
- "if you make the players believe that it is and you don't leave any room for doubt then it's a real optical illusion" ([ByteBlox 2024-06 2:03](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=123))
- "you want your game to be played 24/7 ... you want people to continuously have something to do" ([ByteBlox 2024-06 11:16](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=676))
- "if it's like between RNG like being rare and being hard to get I definitely think people would 100% prefer hard to get" ([CORSO 2024-09 5:19](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=319))
- "A good economy always has money flowing; no one got rich from just saving their money." ([ByteBlox 2024-11 14:30](https://youtu.be/4yIjd9Xn9sE?t=870))
- "it's good to hint towards something scary... I'm not a big fan when games do that... I do like it when games slowly build up to something" ([ByteBlox 2025-01 4:37](https://youtu.be/K-KvVwt_Y4I?t=277))
- "Sometimes you just want to play the game by yourself right that's how it works" ([ByteBlox 2025-01 17:16](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=1036))
- "Cosmetics, the one thing that has never failed anyone ever." ([PineappleDev 2025-03 5:28](https://youtu.be/IEAr0I_sUV4?t=328))
- "People are more worried about losing than they are excited about winning." ([Duckable 2025-03 7:19](https://youtu.be/I9eyeEG6dpc?t=439))
- "The novelty of growing a garden kind of ran out fast when I found out that I have to wait to buy the freaking seeds." ([Cumin 2025-05 7:43](https://youtu.be/E7bedfC8A10?t=463))
- "The trick is that you don't always get what you want, but the chances are always visible." ([janisjanis01 2025-05 1:07](https://youtu.be/Shjq-Xd9Kmw?t=67))
- "When you're about to roll something really rare, your screen starts to zoom out... half a second later, it switches to garbage." ([janisjanis01 2025-05 5:02](https://youtu.be/Shjq-Xd9Kmw?t=302))
- "If your game requires effort before joy, then you're bleeding retention." ([AlvinBlox 2025-06 3:21](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=201))
- "at least that game had attempts at making me feel dopamine. Like, this one, I'm going to freaking die." ([Cumin 2025-08 8:28](https://youtu.be/wdf6cfH3rnA?t=508))
- "Kids are especially susceptible to this micro FOMO, as I'll call it." ([Cumin 2025-11 5:42](https://youtu.be/66n0P2NtBoQ?t=342))
- "When everything is rare, then nothing is. This game was basically only alive because of its economy." ([Cumin 2026-08 16:53](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1013))
- "You can't even get to the next room before the clown is already in chase mode." ([Cumin 2026-08 12:37](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=757))
- "There is no time to breathe in this game." ([Cumin 2026-08 14:58](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=898))
- "Under promise, and deliver." ([rasdev 2026-09 7:27](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=447))
- "All, and I mean all LARP games require improvisation on the spot and context-dependent replies or commands." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/age-based-chat-restrictions-changes/4962382))

## [[ab-testing-and-experiments]]
- "it's the testing is ongoing it's not like no we found out everything about our creative creatives right now" ([GameMakers 2021-11 27:26](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=1646))
- "statsig is often treated like a binary yes or no result... there's a continuum of confidence" ([GameMakers 2022-08 16:19](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=979))
- "don't change a button color on a single screen and expect a statistically meaningful result" ([GameMakers 2022-08 35:51](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=2151))
- "You can't work like in a method of spray and pray." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 42:31](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2551))
- "Conditional Configs let you serve different values for the same config key based on player attributes." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/evolving-experiments-and-analytics-segmentation-experiment-targeting/4828462))
- "Rules are evaluated from top to bottom, and the first matching rule wins." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/evolving-experiments-and-analytics-segmentation-experiment-targeting/4828462))
- "A thumbnail can perform differently depending on the title shown next to it, and vice versa." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ab-testing-experience-titles-together-with-thumbnails/4900092))
- "Do not launch based on early results. Results in the first 24 hours only test for harm." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/experiments-catch-experiment-regressions-faster-with-early-harm-detection/4907734))
- "EHD is only meant to help you decide whether to stop an experiment early. It is not meant for launch decisions." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/experiments-catch-experiment-regressions-faster-with-early-harm-detection/4907734))
- "every time we made a change, we would spend more money on ads, get a more influx of players, and we'd retain like a little bit." ([Roblox Creators 2026-10 15:49](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=949))
